Abstract: We formulate first-order optimization as a minimum-time control problem. The iterate is the state, the update, a combination of the gradients observed so far, is the control, and the points with gradient norm at most a prescribed tolerance form the target set. For a fixed objective and start, the minimum number of oracle queries needed to reach the target is a value function: it measures the complexity of the instance, not the worst case over a class. On a strongly convex quadratic, the conjugate gradient iterates emerge from the discrete Pontryagin conditions, and the value is a controllability index. Beyond the quadratic, a Hessian-generated reachable span replaces the controllability matrix, and feasibility becomes a reachability question: whether a critical point can be reached at all, and in how many steps, is read from the span. Curvature is a resource: a checkable condition certifies that an instance needs fewer steps than the controllability index of its quadratic model at the minimizer, and the gap can grow without bound with the dimension. The value is thus a benchmark for the intrinsic difficulty of each instance, against which any first-order method can be measured.

</details>

Abstract: We establish nondegeneracy and normality conditions for optimal control problems with a single nonregular mixed inequality constraint, where purely finitely additive set functions (charges) appear as multipliers. Abstract: We study clean energy transition planning for campus-scale integrated electricity-heat systems under both strategic level and operational level uncertainties. We formulate a multi-stage stochastic mixed-integer program that jointly optimizes investment and operational decisions for renewable generation, storage, and heat-transfer technologies whose costs and efficiencies evolve stochastically across stages. To account for short-term operational uncertainty, we further derive a robust reformulation based on box uncertainty sets for demand, renewable generation, and heat-transfer performance. To solve the resulting large-scale model, we develop a Benders decomposition algorithm with partial non-anticipativity relaxation. Investment variables and their non-anticipativity constraints are retained in the master problem while operational variables are assigned to scenario-wise subproblems where their non-anticipativity constraints are relaxed. Non-anticipativity of operational variables is restored only at termination through a smaller linear program. We prove that this correction step can only increase the objective function value by a finite bound. We improve the computational performance of the algorithm developed with valid inequalities and a two-phase cut-addition strategy. Using the proposed approach, we solve the Middle East Technical University campus case study at high temporal resolution within a reasonable computational budget, which is not otherwise possible with the extensive form or the classical Benders decomposition. We evaluate the resulting investment plans through rolling-horizon Monte Carlo simulations with an out-of-sample analysis and demonstrate that the added robustness can significantly improve the operational reliability of the transition plans with a moderate increase in total cost.

In our model, the firm chooses an inventory placement across warehouses at the start of the selling horizon. Customers of different types then arrive at discrete time periods over a finite time horizon according to a known distribution. When offered an assortment, each customer type chooses at most one product according to a discrete choice model. The firm then chooses a feasible warehouse from which to fulfill the chosen product, depleting its stock and earning a profit that depends on the product, customer type, and warehouse choice. We develop approximation algorithms and policies with provable theoretical guarantees and strong empirical performance. Under general probabilistic choice models, we establish asymptotically optimal approximation algorithms as warehouse capacities and product supplies scale. Under the multinomial logit (MNL) choice model, we obtain constant-factor approximation algorithms, with approximation factors ranging from 0.080 in the most general setting to 0.199 under stationarity and unlimited product supplies, which means that only warehouse capacity constraints are present. Abstract: We consider a processing network model with $m$ job classes or buffers, exogenous input flows into some classes, $n$ processing activities, and $p$ servers. Each activity is either a specified server processing jobs of a specified class, or a fictional input server delivering jobs of a specified class; jobs change class in Markovian fashion after completing service. A standard multiclass queueing network, with its one-to-one correspondence between job classes and activities, is a special case, but our general model allows two or more ways to process a given class, and some or all input flows may be turned away at the system manager's discretion.
  Costs are linear: a holding cost per time unit for each class $i$ job in the system, and a rejection penalty for each class $i$ arrival denied access $(i=1,\ldots,m)$. The system manager makes input control, job routing, and order-of-service decisions to minimize expected discounted costs over an infinite horizon.

Abstract: Many control problems are classically formulated using integral costs that capture the cumulative performance of a system. Peak or worst-case behavior is captured via supremum or $L^\infty$-costs in another variety of control problems. When both cumulative and peak performance are important, it is natural to consider objective functions that combine the two costs. Although each criterion is well studied, their combination has not been explored extensively and we precisely work on such control problems.
  Towards establishing the existence, we first consider the relaxed framework, where control is considered using probability distributions. Using the well-known compactness and convexity properties of such control spaces, we establish the existence of an optimal relaxed control---we eventually establish the existence of an $ε$-optimal pure (or classical) control, for every $ε> 0.$
  Despite these existence results, computing optimal policies remains challenging due to the non-smoothness introduced by the supremum term, and it is not clear whether the dynamic programming principle holds for our combined problem. To address this, we introduce a family of smooth approximations that yield standard control problems with well-defined optimal (pure) solutions. Using Maximum Theorem, we establish that the solutions of the smooth problems among pure controls form $ε$-optimal for the original problem, with $ε$ tending to zero as the smoothing parameter converges to zero.
  Finally using the methods proposed in this paper, we study a queueing problem to illustrate (among others) that the required trade-off between peak congestion levels and cumulative performance can be achieved.

</details>

Abstract: We study optimal control problems for incompressible fluids in two-dimensional domains with a cold boundary and internal heat sources and sinks. Given a kinetic energy budget, measured by the square of a nondimensional Péclet number $\mathrm{Pe}$, the goal is to maximize a cooling efficiency inversely proportional to the mean square gradient of the fluid's temperature. Using Lagrange duality, we formulate a well-posed dual problem whose solution yields an upper bound on the maximum cooling efficiency $\mathcal{E}(\mathrm{Pe})$. We then numerically approximate the dual problem using a convergent hierarchy of semidefinite programs obtained via discretization. We illustrate this computational approach on optimal cooling problems in a square and in an annulus, explaining also how problem symmetries can be exploited to reduce computational complexity. Finally, we construct admissible points for the dual problem to obtain new analytical upper bounds on the optimal cooling efficiency $\mathcal{E}(\mathrm{Pe})$. Specifically, we prove that $\mathcal{E}(\mathrm{Pe}) \lesssim \mathrm{Pe}^{2}$ for arbitrary domains and heat distributions, and that $\mathcal{E}(\mathrm{Pe})\lesssim \mathrm{Pe}^{2}/ \ln^2\mathrm{Pe}$ for cooling flows in disks and annuli with heat source/sink distributions with a positive azimuthal average. These results generalize and improve known efficiency bounds for energy-constrained cooling flows in a disk.

Abstract: Wasserstein distributionally robust optimization (DRO) is a versatile and widely adopted framework for decision-making under uncertainty, yet its standard deterministic reformulations generally contain non-convex inner subproblems that are challenging to solve. To address this issue, we propose a shrinkage path heuristic that reduces the solution of a DRO problem to a one-dimensional search over the line segment connecting the (typically benign) sample average approximation (SAA) and the (more demanding but practically solvable) classical robust optimization solution. We derive a priori suboptimality bounds in stylized settings and, for the general case, a posteriori bounds obtained by applying a similar heuristic to a dual formulation. Numerical experiments on a multi-item newsvendor and an appointment scheduling problem show that the shrinkage path heuristic attains 85-110% (resp. 45-70%) of the out-of-sample performance improvements of Wasserstein DRO over SAA, at a fraction of the computational cost.

Abstract: The alternating direction method of multipliers (ADMM), as a landmark algorithm, has attracted tremendous research attention and extensive practical applications over the past two decades. It is well known that, although the two-block ADMM enjoys well-established theoretical convergence guarantees, its direct extension to the three-block case may fail to converge, as demonstrated by existing counterexamples [5]. In this paper, we give a negative answer: direct three-block ADMM may fail even when the first two blocks are strongly convex quadratics. Using Codex with GPT-5.6 Sol, we construct an explicit rational counterexample candidate and verify it along a piecewise-affine reduction path; exact checks show that direct three-block ADMM on this instance produces a bounded nonconvergent orbit of period 66. Within the same Codex workflow, we further guide a study of multiplier relaxation and clarify when convergence can be restored at the fixed-instance and class levels: a problem-dependent small dual step can restore convergence, whereas no positive relative step works uniformly over the whole class. Furthermore, we also test the recent Kimi Code with Kimi K3 model without the Codex candidate or project-specific route guidance; along a different path it produces an exact locally attracting period-23 certificate, convertible to an equivalent all-identity instance. The comparison suggests that different research-harness configurations can shape the mathematical objects explored and the certificates pursued.

Abstract: Differentiable solver layers embed constrained optimization into end-to-end learning systems, but fixed-depth unrolling must trade off solution quality, feasibility, and computational budget. We propose Residual-Controlled Douglas--Rachford Splitting (RCDRS), a differentiable solver layer for conic linear programs. RCDRS treats an unrolled solver as a feedback-controlled dynamical system, where a causal controller adapts the relaxation and objective-drive parameters while preserving the projection-splitting structure of Douglas--Rachford splitting. Theoretically, we show that each fixed admissible block remains an averaged relaxed DRS operator and admits finite-step fixed-point residual bounds. We further analyze safeguarded time-varying rollouts as summable perturbations of a limiting averaged operator, and recover terminal primal-dual diagnostics from the final splitting state. Experiments on mixed-cone benchmarks and engineering applications show that RCDRS improves solution quality, feasibility and downstream decision performance. Abstract: In this paper, a quantitative condition for optimality for distributed optimal control problems with box-constraints that are subject to a semilinear elliptic equation is considered. An important property of the investigated optimal control problems is the absence of a Tikhonov regularization. It is well known that at a given control, the second variation is a quadratic form in the linearized state, and its curvature coefficient function may vanish or change sign. We present a quantitative condition that implies coercivity with respect to the $L^2$-norm of the linearized-states. As a consequence, the stability of the second-order condition under perturbations of the states and the tracking data is shown.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证金融市场吸收公共信息的速度这一基本问题，具体检验新闻价格变动是发生在发布前/发布时（如市场谚语所言）还是发布后，以及不同新闻类型和幅度上的差异。

Method: 使用大语言模型教师通过主动学习蒸馏为紧凑分类器，为每篇文章标注17种事件标签和5种属性；将文章聚类为故事以区分首次报道和后续报道；计算beta调整的异常收益，覆盖2023-2026年约3000只美国股票的457万篇新闻，共168万个股票日事件，并以364,405个中性情绪事件作为安慰剂对照组。

Result: 三个核心发现：(1) 价格变动集中在发布前和发布日，发布日收盘时的累计涨幅是20天后的2.8倍，谣言事件在谣言日已捕获全部涨幅；(2) 市场对量化基本面新闻（盈利、股息、指引、分析师行动）反应不足，持续数周漂移，而对软性新闻（发布、宏观评论、领导层变动）过度反应后回吐；(3) 新闻提高了发布前的波动率，发布后波动率下降因为不确定性得到解决。研究还生成了各事件标签的漂移表。

Conclusion: 市场谚语总体得到验证，但不同新闻类型存在系统性差异：硬数据型新闻市场反应不足、软新闻市场反应过度。研究提供了新闻条件预测模型可用的先验信息。

Abstract: Two old market sayings hold that news is already priced in by the time it is published, and that the rumor is bought while the news is sold. Both place the price move associated with a piece of news before and at publication rather than after it. Whether the claims hold, for which kinds of news, and by how much are basic questions about how fast markets absorb public information. We test them on 4.57 million financial news articles covering roughly 3,000 US stocks (2023-2026). A large language model teacher, distilled into a compact classifier through active learning, assigns each article one of 17 event tags and five attributes; articles are clustered into stories to separate first reports from follow-up coverage; and beta-adjusted abnormal returns are measured around the resulting 1.68 million stock-day events, with 364,405 neutral-sentiment events as a placebo group. Three results follow. First, the price move associated with news concentrates before and at publication: pooled across all signed events, the cumulative move in the news direction by the close of publication day is 2.8 times its value 20 days later, and for rumor-flagged events the rumor day captures the entire move while the subsequent confirmation contributes nothing. Second, measured against the placebo of comparable stocks, markets underreact to numbers and overreact to stories: quantified fundamental news (earnings, dividends, guidance, analyst actions) keeps drifting in the direction of the news for weeks, while soft story-driven news (launches, macro commentary, leadership) gives back its move. Third, news carries width as well as direction: publicity raises volatility before the publication day, and volatility declines once the news is out, because publication resolves uncertainty. The study also produces a table of measured drift for each event tag, usable as a prior in news-conditioned forecasting models.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模评估语言模型智能体越来越依赖于用第二个语言模型作为自动评判器（judge），因为真实环境奖励昂贵、缓慢或部署时不可用。然而现有方法要么手工编写评分标准（如G-Eval），要么微调评判器权重，两者都倾向于给流畅但失败的轨迹评成功。需要一个可靠、可归因、无需环境访问的评判方法。

Method: RubricForge通过反射式进化（reflective evolution）针对少量带真实标签的轨迹迭代优化评分标准，以最大化与环境奖励的一致性；生成的标准被冻结后，在单次模型调用中无环境访问地应用于留出轨迹。优化产物是人类可读文本，每条判定可归因到具名标准。

Result: 使用同一个冻结的7B模型作为智能体和评判器，在tau-bench（从220条rollout中抽取的173条标记轨迹）和WebShop（160条）上：与通用G-Eval相比，总体一致性无统计显著差异（McNemar p=0.248），绝对分数校准略差（|err|差-0.048，p=2x10^-4）；但错误通过率约为一半（tau-bench上0.115 vs 0.173），且WebShop分级更忠实（Spearman 0.410 vs 0.370）。

Conclusion: 对于无奖励（reward-free）的评估器，错误通过率比整体一致性更关键，因为错误通过会部署有缺陷的智能体，而错误拒绝仅浪费一次重试。RubricForge通过降低错误通过率、提供可归因的判定，为部署场景提供了更有价值的评估方案。

Abstract: Evaluating language-model agents at scale increasingly relies on a second language model as an automatic judge, because the gold signal, an executable environment reward, is expensive, slow, or unavailable at deployment time. Such a judge is a reward-free proxy whose value depends on whether it can be trusted, yet existing judges either hand-write the scoring rubric, as in G-Eval, or fine-tune the judge's weights, and both tend to credit fluent but unsuccessful trajectories as successes. We instead induce the text of an agent-judging rubric from a small set of ground-truth-labeled trajectories, grounding it in true outcomes. We present RubricForge, which evolves a judge rubric by reflective evolution against labeled trajectories to maximize agreement with the environment reward, freezes it, and applies it to held-out trajectories in one model call with no environment access. The optimized artifact is human-readable text, so every verdict is attributable to named criteria. Using one frozen 7B model as both agent and judge, on tau-bench (173 labeled trajectories drawn from 220 rollouts) and WebShop (160), the principal gain is faithfulness rather than raw agreement. The edge over a generic G-Eval judge is not statistically significant (McNemar p = 0.248), and absolute-score calibration marginally favors the generic judge (|err| difference -0.048, p = 2x10^-4). Yet RubricForge over-credits failed trajectories roughly half as often (0.115 vs. 0.173 false-pass rate on tau-bench, with three over-credit catches and zero reversals) and ranks graded WebShop outcomes more faithfully (Spearman 0.410 vs. 0.370). For a reward-free evaluator the false-pass rate, not aggregate agreement, is the deployment-relevant quantity, since a false pass ships a broken agent whereas a false fail merely costs a retry.

</details>

*Pradeep Kumar Sharma,Shantanu Godbole,Hritvik Shrivastava*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对Qwen3.6-35B-A3B模型（40个MoE层，每层256个专家，top-8路由）进行了系统性层敏感度分析，利用基于幅度的专家掩码在XLCoST跨语言代码翻译基准上评估。核心发现是层敏感度具有强深度依赖性：早期和中期层对专家掩码高度脆弱，而后期层（尤其35-39层）能容忍激进的低幅度专家掩码。后期聚焦策略在掩码640个专家的同时保持优秀输出质量，为深度感知的MoE专家压缩提供了实证基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE架构在大规模语言模型中广泛使用，但各MoE层的相对重要性尚未被充分刻画，尤其是在模型压缩场景下。理解哪些层可以安全地掩码专家、哪些层必须保留，对有效的模型压缩和权重剪枝至关重要。

Method: 对Qwen3.6-35B-A3B模型采用基于幅度的专家掩码策略，在XLCoST跨语言代码翻译基准上开展多阶段研究（100/300/500提示规模），在三台H100 GPU服务器上系统性地逐层评估专家掩码的影响，同时测试了不同层选择策略（全层掩码、后期聚焦、极后期窄带策略）以及top-k路由宽度缩减（8→6）的组合效果。

Result: 早期层（0-9）和中期层（10-29）对专家掩码高度敏感；后期层（30-39）尤其极后期层（35-39）能容忍激进掩码。全层30%掩码仅保留150/300个好结果，而后期聚焦策略在掩码640-1,145个专家的同时保留249-255/300。500提示验证集上，窄带极后期策略（35-39层@50%）保留419/500并仅掩码640/10,240个专家。将top-k从8减至6可在100提示探针上获得显著墙钟时间减少且无质量损失，但暂不能与激进专家掩码干净组合。

Conclusion: 层敏感度呈现强深度依赖模式，这为深度感知的MoE专家掩码提供了实证基础，为物理权重手术、基于激活的专家评分和基于训练的恢复等后续工作指明了实用路径。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures scale large language models (LLMs) while preserving computational efficiency through sparse activation. Despite their widespread adoption, the relative importance of individual MoE layers remains insufficiently characterized, particularly for model compression. This paper presents a systematic layer-wise sensitivity analysis of the Qwen3.6-35B-A3B model (40 MoE layers, 256 experts per layer, top-8 routing) using magnitude-based expert masking on the XLCoST cross-lingual code translation benchmark. We conduct a multi-phase study spanning 100, 300, and 500 prompt evaluation scales across three H100 GPU servers. Our central finding is that layer sensitivity is strongly depth-dependent: early layers (0-9) and middle layers (10-29) are highly fragile to expert masking, while late layers (30-39), and especially very-late layers (35-39), tolerate aggressive masking of low-magnitude experts. Flat all-layer masking at 30% retains only 150/300 Good+Similar outputs at 300-prompt scale, whereas late-focused policies retain 249-255/300 while masking 640-1,145 experts. On a later 500-prompt held-out validation slice, the narrow very-late policy (layers 35-39 @ 50%) achieves the strongest quality/masked-expert tradeoff among tested candidates, retaining 419/500 Good+Similar outputs while masking only 640 of 10,240 total experts. We additionally characterize top-k routing width reduction from 8 to 6 active experts per token, which shows a large observed wall-clock reduction on a 100-prompt probe with no Good+Similar loss, though it does not yet compose cleanly with aggressive expert masking. These findings provide an empirical foundation for depth-aware MoE expert masking and establish a practical path toward physical weight surgery, activation-based expert scoring, and training-based recovery.

</details>

*Masahiro Kato,Taka Kato*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了大型语言模型应用中"会话交接"（session handover）的方法论和理论性质，将交接形式化为任务相关上下文学习（ICL）状态的转移，区分了早期材料的精确恢复与目标分布的保持，并提出了一个三部分记录方案来指导会话间应传递哪些信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当LLM应用的上下文达到模型输入限制、应用重启或由另一代理继续任务时，需要决定从先前会话传递哪些信息。目前缺乏关于交接的理论框架来指导这一决策。

Method: 将交接形式化为任务相关ICL状态的转移；在外生条件下，用预测等价性刻画最粗糙的确定性充分交接，并给出固定长度比特需求；提出三部分记录方案（精确存储决策和约束、使用任务合理性统计量处理重复证据、保留统计量无法保持效果的原始观测）；在Gaussian线性回归和非参数回归中分别推导精确有限维交接和记忆-预测误差的界限。

Result: Gaussian线性回归给出精确有限维交接和有限比特扰动界；非参数回归给出将记忆与平方预测误差联系起来的上下界；分析隔离了记忆约束、写入者和延续过程的效应，并量化了在真实下游查询未知前写入的成本。

Conclusion: 该研究为"交接必须保留什么"以及"记忆需求如何依赖于延续任务"提供了理论基础和方法论指导。

Abstract: This study investigates the methodological and theoretical properties of session handover in applications that use large language models. A task may continue in a new session when the context reaches the model's input limit, when the application restarts, or when another agent is asked to finish the task. The application must then decide which information from the earlier session to pass on. We formulate handover as the transfer of a task-relative in-context learning (ICL) state and distinguish exact recovery of earlier material from preservation of the target distribution. Under an exogeneity condition, predictive equivalence characterizes the coarsest deterministic sufficient handover and gives a fixed-length bit requirement. The analysis isolates the effects of the memory constraint, the writer, and the continuation procedure, and quantifies the cost of writing before the realized downstream query is known. We propose a three-part record that stores decisions and constraints exactly, uses task-justified statistics for repeated evidence, and retains original observations whose effect is not preserved by those statistics. Gaussian linear regression gives an exact finite-dimensional handover and finite-bit perturbation bounds, while nonparametric regression gives upper and lower bounds that relate memory to squared prediction error. These results provide a theory and method for deciding what a handover must retain and how its memory requirement depends on the continuation task.

</details>


### [52] [Modular Cognitive Architecture Emerges in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13567)
*Pengrui Han,Jacob Andreas,Evelina Fedorenko,Andrea Gregor de Varda*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过电路分析，在46个任务（涵盖语言、形式推理、社会推理、物理推理四个认知领域）中检验大型语言模型（LLMs）是否像人脑一样发展出模块化架构。研究发现LLMs确实形成了与人脑相似的功能模块化组织：人脑中共享同一网络的任务在LLM中会招募重叠的神经元，而不同网络的任务则招募不同的神经元，表明模块化可能是智能系统的普遍属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人脑展现出显著的功能专门化组织，不同网络分别支持语言、形式推理、心智推理和物理推理。核心问题是：这种模块化组织是智能系统构建的基本原则，还是生物大脑特有的进化偶然事件？作者希望通过检验LLMs（通过完全不同的优化过程创造的智能系统）是否表现出类似组织来回答这一问题。

Method: 使用电路分析（circuit analyses）方法，在N=46个任务上进行实验，这些任务涵盖四个认知领域（语言、形式推理、社交推理、物理推理）。通过比较LLMs中神经元招募模式与人脑网络组织之间的对应关系来检验模块化结构是否出现。

Result: 研究发现LLMs发展出与人脑镜像的模块化架构：在人脑中依赖相同网络的任务会招募LLMs中重叠的神经元，而依赖不同网络的任务则招募不同的神经元。LLMs与人脑之间表现出清晰的模块化对应关系。

Conclusion: 模块化在大脑和神经网络中的趋同涌现表明，功能模块化可能是智能系统的一个基本属性，而非生物大脑特有的偶然特性。这一发现对理解智能系统（无论是生物的还是人工的）的通用组织原则具有重要意义。

Abstract: The human brain exhibits a striking degree of functional specialization, with distinct networks supporting language, formal reasoning, reasoning about other minds, and reasoning about the physical world. Is this modular organization a fundamental principle of how intelligent systems must be built, or an evolutionary accident specific to biological brains? Here, we test whether a similar organization emerges in Large Language Models--another class of intelligent systems created through a very different optimization process. Using circuit analyses across N=46 tasks spanning four cognitive domains (language, formal reasoning, social reasoning, physical reasoning), we find that LLMs develop a modular architecture that mirrors the human brain: tasks drawing on the same network in humans recruit overlapping neurons in LLMs, whereas tasks drawing on different networks recruit distinct neurons. The convergent emergence of modularity in brains and neural networks suggests that it may be a fundamental property of intelligent systems.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM服务负载研究在规模和范围上有限，通常观察时间短，对用户在真实生产环境中与模型的交互方式可见性不足，无法充分捕捉负载随时间演变的过程以及用户-模型交互对生产流量的塑造作用。

Method: 从Chutes获取一年期的LLM服务生产追踪数据，该数据涵盖多个模型和用户（包括热门和长尾模型），并从聚合、时间、模型级和用户级四个视角全面分析负载特征。

Conclusion: 作者将发布完整的一年期追踪数据，使后续研究能够在无需依赖采样或合成工作负载的情况下进行生产行为研究，为LLM服务系统的测评和优化提供更真实的数据基础。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) serving has become a critical cloud workload, and realistic traces are essential for motivating and benchmarking serving systems. However, existing LLM serving workload studies remain limited in scale and scope. They often observe short time periods and provide limited visibility into how users interact with models in production. As a result, they do not fully capture how LLM serving workloads evolve over time or how user-model interactions shape production traffic.
  In this work, we further the understanding of real-world LLM serving workloads through both a global characterization and a longitudinal study of a one-year production trace from Chutes. Unlike prior studies, our trace captures full production behavior across many models and users, including both popular and long-tail models. We analyze the workload from aggregate, temporal, model-level, and user-level perspectives, revealing workload evolution and user-model structure that are typically hidden behind aggregate views. To support future research, we will release the full one-year trace with the paper, enabling downstream studies of production behavior without relying on sampled or synthetically generated workloads.

</details>


### [54] [Sensor-Driven Mission Synthesis for UAV/UGV Swarms: A TB-CSPN Coordination Architecture with Hardware-Enforced Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14306)
*Uwe M. Borghoff,Paolo Bottoni,Remo Pareschi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种异构无人机/无人地面车（UAV/UGV）群体的协调架构，该架构从不完整、多模态的传感器证据中综合任务动作，同时在执行边界通过硬件强制的安全性加以保护。架构结合雷达、射频、声学和视觉观测与基于主题的通信空间Petri网（TB-CSPN）编排，支持在部分且不断演变的信息下进行增量任务构建。顾问代理将传感器输出转化为带时间界限的语义令牌，监督代理则提供授权和策略管控的任务转换发布。这种解释、协调与执行相分离的设计产生可审计的决策路径，并通过守卫与同步约束协调层的非确定性，实现异构证据的有界时间整合。为增强在对抗环境（包括网络攻击、欺骗、干扰和通信丢失）中的韧性，数字协调层辅以独立的模拟安全包络，对车辆不安全执行器命令进行限幅或否决。沿海监视案例研究展示了所提架构在操作不确定性下实现可靠、受治理且物理安全的群体协调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构UAV/UGV群体在对抗和不确定环境中面临多模态传感器数据融合、任务动态形成与物理安全保证之间的核心矛盾。现有方法难以同时满足可审计的决策路径、有界时间证据整合以及面对网络与通信攻击时的硬件级安全保障。

Method: 提出分层架构：1) 顾问代理将雷达、RF、声学、视觉等多模态传感器数据转换为带时间界限的语义令牌；2) TB-CSPN（基于主题的通信空间Petri网）作为协调层，通过守卫和同步约束非确定性，实现增量任务编排；3) 监督代理负责授权与策略管控的任务转换；4) 独立模拟安全包络在驱动层限幅或否决不安全命令。

Result: 通过沿海监视案例研究验证了架构能在操作不确定性下实现可靠、受治理且物理安全的群体协调，证明了解释、协调与执行分离可产生可审计的决策路径，并能在对抗环境（网络攻击、欺骗、干扰、通信丢失）下保持安全性。

Conclusion: 将数字协调层与独立模拟安全包络相结合，可在保持决策灵活性与治理能力的同时，为群体系统提供硬件强制的物理安全底线，适用于对抗环境中的关键任务。

Abstract: This paper presents a coordination architecture for heterogeneous UAV/UGV swarms that synthesises mission actions from uncertain, multi-modal sensor evidence while preserving hardware-enforced safety at the actuation boundary. The approach combines radar, RF, acoustic, and visual observations with Topic-Based Communication Space Petri Net (TB-CSPN) orchestration to support incremental mission formation under partial and evolving information. Consultant agents transform sensor outputs into temporally bounded semantic tokens, while supervisor agents provide authorisation and policy-governed release of mission transitions. This separation between interpretation, coordination, and execution yields auditable decision paths, constrains non-determinism within the coordination layer through guards and synchronisation, and enables bounded-time integration of heterogeneous evidence. To improve resilience in contested environments, including cyber compromise, spoofing, jamming, and communication loss, the digital coordination layer is complemented by independent analogue safety envelopes that clamp or veto unsafe actuator commands issued to individual vehicles. A coastal-surveillance case study illustrates how the proposed architecture enables dependable, governed, and physically safe swarm coordination under operational uncertainty.

</details>


### [55] [Agentao: A Governed Local-First Runtime for Tool-Using LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13574)
*Bo Jin,Qiang Jiao,Xin Tong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Agentao，一个面向工具调用型LLM代理的受治理的本地优先运行时系统。它将模型生成的行动提案与主机授权的执行分离，通过分层架构实现权限、状态、协议边界和执行轨迹的显式运行时抽象，使代理更加可治理、可检查和适合主机控制的本地环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理的能力不断增强（调用工具、修改本地状态、使用持久内存、交互外部协议），但同时也引入了过度权限操作、弱可审计性、提示注入、工具投毒和不受控副作用等安全风险。现有系统缺乏对代理运行时行为和资源访问的系统性治理机制。

Method: Agentao采用分层架构，将模型生成的行动提案与主机授权的执行分离。系统包含主机面对界面、主机契约、运行时核心、权限中介工具系统，以及支持内存、重放、插件、技能、子代理和协议集成的子系统。该方法不提供形式化的安全保证，而是通过显式化的权限、状态、协议边界和执行轨迹等运行时抽象来增强代理的可治理性和可检查性。

Result: 论文提出了Agentao的完整设计，包括动机、威胁模型、设计目标、治理模型、执行流水线和结构化事件接口。系统代码已公开在GitHub上可供使用和验证。

Conclusion: Agentao表明，通过将治理相关的概念（权限、状态、协议边界、执行轨迹）作为显式的运行时抽象，可以构建更加可治理、可检查且适合主机控制本地环境的LLM代理系统，但这并非提供正式的安全保证，而是展示一种实用的治理范式。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly operate as execution systems that invoke tools, modify local state, use persistent memory, and interact with external protocols. These capabilities make agents useful, but they also introduce risks related to over-privileged actions, weak auditability, prompt injection, tool poisoning, and uncontrolled side effects. This paper presents Agentao, a governed local-first runtime for tool-using LLM agents. Agentao separates model-generated action proposals from host-authorized execution through a layered architecture consisting of host-facing surfaces, a host contract, a runtime core, a permission-mediated tool system, and supporting subsystems for memory, replay, plugins, skills, sub-agents, and protocol integration. We describe the motivation, threat model, design goals, governance model, execution pipeline, and structured event interface of the system. Agentao does not provide formal safety guarantees; rather, it demonstrates how permissions, state, protocol boundaries, and execution traces can be made explicit runtime abstractions for building agents that are more governable, inspectable, and suitable for host-controlled local environments. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/jin-bo/agentao.

</details>


### [56] [AI Evaluation Should Work With Humans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13577)
*Jan Kulveit,Gavin Leech,Tomáš Gavenčiak,Raymond Douglas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇立场论文，主张当前以"超人自主表现"为核心的AI评估范式正将AI发展引向错误方向，应转向评估人机协作团队的整体绩效，以促进AI与人类能力真正互补，带来更优的社会结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者认为当前AI评估的主流范式聚焦于超人级别的自主表现，隐含地以"替代人类"为目标，这正在将AI发展引向错误方向。这种评估导向可能会误导AI研发，使之忽视与人类协作的价值。

Method: 本文采用立场论证的方式，通过批判现有AI评估范式的局限性，提出应将评估重点转向人机团队（human-AI teams）的整体绩效，以此作为AI发展的导向。

Result: 作者论证了这种协作导向的转变将促进AI系统成为人类能力的真正补充（true complements），从而相比当前的评估路径产生远更好的社会结果。

Conclusion: AI社区应当从评估"替代人类"的自主表现转向评估"人机协作"的团队绩效，这一转变将对AI发展方向和社会福祉产生积极影响。

Abstract: This position paper argues that the dominant paradigm of AI evaluation (which focuses on superhuman autonomous performance and so implicitly targets the goal of replacing humans) is guiding AI development in the wrong direction. Instead, the AI community should pivot to evaluating the performance of human--AI teams. We argue that this collaborative shift will foster AI systems that act as true complements to human capabilities and therefore lead to far better societal outcomes than will the current process.

</details>


### [57] [Stable Miscalibration in Large Language Models: A Practical View of High-Confidence Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13591)
*Akira Okutomi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了大语言模型中高置信度错误的一种新解释——稳定误校准（stable miscalibration），即置信但错误的答案在小扰动下仍然局部稳定，而非传统认为的脆弱内部推理。作者结合输出级审计分数与内部敏感性探针两种诊断方法，发现自我批判式提示能减少隐藏层敏感性，支持提示诱导的局部稳定化，但部分高置信度错误可能既是稳定的又是误校准的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高置信度的错误答案常被视为模型内部推理脆弱的证据。作者质疑这一默认假设，提出另一种可能性：某些高置信度的错误是稳定的误校准，即在微小扰动下依然保持稳定的错误答案，而非仅仅表现为内部推理脆弱。

Method: 提出两种互补的诊断方法：（1）标签感知的输出级审计分数，基于强制回答基线，对领域按置信度变化和过度自信错误进行排序；（2）内部敏感性探针，测量隐藏状态的运动。在多领域二元事实审计集上，结合这两种方法来评估自我批判式提示的效果，并在三个开放权重模型中跨层检验隐藏状态敏感性变化。

Result: 审计分数能够追踪自我批判如何降低决策损失，但直接带标签基线在排名增益上更强。内部结果显示，自我批判式提示在三个开放权重模型中一致降低了各层隐藏状态敏感性，支持提示诱导的局部稳定化而非纯输出级弃权模式。然而，审计定义的高置信错误并未明显比置信的正确答案更局部敏感，说明部分高置信错误是稳定且误校准的，而非单纯脆弱的。

Conclusion: 自我批判式提示导致的错误减少主要是由局部稳定化（内部隐藏状态敏感性下降）驱动的，而非简单的输出级弃权行为。但这也意味着模型存在稳定的误校准：一些高置信度的错误答案在扰动下依然稳定，不能被简单视为内部推理的脆弱表现，这对理解和改进LLM可靠性具有重要启示。

Abstract: High-confidence errors in large language models are often treated as evidence of fragile internal inference. We study a different possibility: stable miscalibration, where a confident wrong answer remains locally stable under small perturbations. We combine two diagnostics: a label-aware output-level audit score that ranks domains by confidence variation and overconfident mistakes under a forced-answer baseline, and an internal sensitivity probe that measures hidden-state movement. On a multi-domain binary factual audit set, this audit score tracks where abstention-aware self-critique reduces decision loss, although direct labeled baselines rank the same gain more strongly. Internally, self-critical prompting consistently reduces hidden-state sensitivity across layers in three open-weight models. This supports prompt-induced local stabilization rather than a purely output-level abstention pattern, but it does not imply calibration: audit-defined overconfident errors are not clearly more locally sensitive than confidently correct answers, so some high-confidence errors may be stable and miscalibrated rather than simply fragile.

</details>


### [58] [Implementing Computational Law in Wolfram Language for the Governance of Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13958)
*James K. Wiles*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨如何治理无法完全审查其推理过程的AI系统，提出治理无需理解推理，只需明确系统义务、许可和禁止的行为并检查其合规性。作者在Wolfram语言中实现了Reified Input/Output Logic（DAPRECO知识库背后的形式化方法），测试GPT-4将英文法律语句翻译为该形式化的能力并报告失败案例，通过AI守卫犬案例展示形式化规则如何从合同直接延伸到具身代理的操作代码中，产生可审计的符号化行为理由。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统日益复杂，其推理过程往往无法完全审查，这给治理带来根本性挑战。传统的治理方法依赖于理解系统内部运作，但这一前提在不可审查的AI推理面前失效。作者提出治理的重新概念化：不要求理解推理，只要求明确行为边界并验证合规性。这一视角转换可能使AI治理从监控内部思维转向外部行为合规检查。

Method: 作者在Wolfram语言中实现了Reified Input/Output Logic形式化框架，包括核心I/O公理、义务、许可、构成性规范、具体化事件性和时间算子。然后设计实验测试GPT-4将英文法律语句翻译为该形式化的能力，系统记录翻译失败模式（幻觉函数、遗漏时间范围、偏离形式化、静默编码错误规范）。最后通过AI守卫犬案例研究，演示形式化规则如何从计算合同直接嵌入具身代理的运营代码。

Result: GPT-4翻译法律文本为形式化逻辑时存在多种失败模式：产生幻觉函数、遗漏时间范围、偏离形式化规范，最严重的是代码能运行且读起来合理但静默编码了错误规范。案例研究表明形式化规则可以成功从合同延伸到代理操作代码中，产生符号化、可审计的行为理由。

Conclusion: 计算法（computational law）可以作为AI治理工具，将法律以可编程方式形式化是一个值得追求的目标。治理AI不一定需要理解其内部推理，通过形式化行为规范并验证合规性即可实现有效治理。但当前AI翻译法律文本为形式化的可靠性不足，需要更稳健的方法确保形式化的正确性。

Abstract: How do we govern AI systems whose reasoning we cannot fully inspect? Governance does not require understanding a system's reasoning. It requires stating what the system is obliged, permitted, and forbidden to do, and checking whether it complied. I present an implementation of Reified Input/Output Logic, the formalism behind the DAPRECO knowledge base, in Wolfram Language: the core I/O axioms, obligations, permissions, constitutive norms, reified eventualities, and temporal operators. I then test whether GPT-4 can translate English legal statements into the formalism, and report the failures: hallucinated functions, omitted temporal scope, deviation from the formalism, and (in the worst cases) code that runs, reads plausibly, but silently encodes the wrong norm. A case study, an AI guard dog operating under a computational contract, shows how formalized rules can extend from a contract directly into the operational code of an embodied agent, producing symbolic, auditable justifications for its behaviour. I argue that computational law can be used as a governance tool and that a desirable goal would be to formalize the law that can and ought to be programmatically executable.

</details>


### [59] [Measuring Cross-Task Behavioral Consistency in Language Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13598)
*Amritesh Banerjee,Pranil Raichura*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出行为一致性指标(BCM)，通过训练模型从智能体执行轨迹的行为特征预测任务成功，计算每个轨迹的特征归因向量，并衡量系统内向量间的平均成对相似度，用以量化智能体跨任务的行为一致性。基于约9000条轨迹的实验表明，跨任务一致性与任务内一致性是不同维度，且一致性无法简化为成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体评估几乎完全依赖结果指标（如成功率），只能反映智能体是否成功，无法反映其行为的一致性。作者认为行为一致性是独立且可衡量的属性，需要在结果指标之外补充过程层面的可靠性信号。

Method: BCM方法包括：(1) 从智能体执行轨迹中提取行为特征；(2) 训练模型预测任务成功；(3) 计算每条轨迹的特征归因向量；(4) 衡量同一智能体系统内这些向量的平均成对相似度作为一致性得分。

Result: 在约9000条来自六种语言模型智能体的软件工程任务轨迹上，发现跨任务一致性与任务内一致性是可分离的不同维度：有些系统在重复任务上局部可复现但全局碎片化，有些则在两个尺度上都一致。一致性无法由成功率推导，成功率相近的系统一致性可能差异很大；在控制任务难度的任务内对照下，前沿模型与开源模型的一致性差距仍然存在。

Conclusion: BCM可作为过程层面的可靠性信号，补充传统的结果指标，并提供跨任务与任务内一致性分离的新视角。作者也明确说明了该指标适用的条件边界。

Abstract: Agent evaluation relies almost entirely on outcome metrics such as success rate, which capture whether an agent succeeds but not how consistently it behaves. We argue that behavioral consistency across tasks is a distinct and measurable property, and we introduce the Behavioral Consistency Metric (BCM) to quantify it. BCM trains a model to predict task success from behavioral features of agent execution traces, derives a per-trajectory feature-attribution vector, and measures the mean pairwise similarity of these vectors within an agent system. Across roughly 9,000 trajectories from six language model agents on software engineering tasks, our central finding is that cross-task and within-task consistency are distinct axes that can diverge: some systems are locally reproducible, behaving similarly on repeated attempts at one task, yet globally fragmented, with no stable strategy across different tasks, while others are consistent at both scales. Prior work measures only same-task reproducibility and so cannot observe this separation. We further find that consistency is not reducible to success rate, since systems with comparable success can differ sharply in consistency, and that the frontier-versus-open-source consistency gap persists under a within-task control that holds task difficulty constant. We position BCM as a process-level reliability signal that complements outcome metrics, and we are explicit about the conditions under which it is meaningful.

</details>


### [60] [Cross-Disciplinary Taxonomy and Modeling of Misunderstanding Generation, Amplification, and Detection, from Pragmatics to AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13604)
*Babak Abbaschian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文从九个不常互相引用的研究领域整合关于误解的文献，将误解分析为一个分层过程（分歧产生→放大→检测修复或持续存在），识别出11种精确失败模式，并将其映射到8个分析层。作者对这些层进行了形式化建模，扩展了信息与传播理论，从信号传输扩展到意义重构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着沟通从面对面实时交互转向AI中介渠道，沟通者失去了对话修复（repair）所依赖的资源，而新的误解检测手段尚未跟上。误解检测成为亟待解决的问题，但缺乏一个统一、可审计的分类框架来定位误解的机制。

Method: 作者整合了九个通常互不引用的研究领域的文献，识别出11种精确失败模式；依据每种模式在沟通过程中的特定作用点推导出8个分析层（而非采用现有模型）；对8个层进行形式化建模，扩展信息与传播理论；并提供逐源证据矩阵、编码手册和9个对话案例作为可审计性支撑。

Result: 11种机制中，8种主要产生分歧，2种主要放大已有分歧，1种决定分歧是否被检测和修复。该分类是首个既能在过程中定位误解机制、又能按功能进行分类的方案。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了首个整合性的误解分类框架，同时具备过程定位和功能分类能力，并为AI中介沟通环境下的误解检测提供了理论和方法基础。

Abstract: Detection of misunderstanding is an urgent problem to solve because communication has moved away from real-time, in-person interaction and is increasingly handled by AI-mediated channels. This shift cuts communicators off from the resources repair depends on faster than new means of detection are being built. In this paper we analyse misunderstanding as a layered process in which a divergence is generated, may then be amplified, and is either detected and repaired or left to persist unnoticed. Consolidating accounts from nine fields of research that do not ordinarily cite one another, we identify eleven exact failure modes and show that each operates at a specific point in a communicative process rather than anywhere within it. Those points give eight analytical layers, derived from the literature rather than adopted from an existing model. Eight of the mechanisms primarily generate a divergence, two primarily amplify one already present, and one governs whether a divergence is detected and repaired. We model the eight layers formally, extending information and communication theory from the transmission of signals to the reconstruction of meaning, and we supply a source-by-source evidence matrix that makes every rating auditable, a coding manual, and nine analysed dialogue cases. No prior classification of misunderstanding both locates mechanisms at points in the process and types them by function.

</details>


### [61] [Active Perception for Embodied Disambiguation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13605)
*Yiwei Liu,Luwei Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向具身目标消歧的主动感知框架，以主动观察为核心信息获取手段，利用视觉-语言模型基于累积的视觉证据和交互信息，动态决策是继续观察、请求澄清还是完成目标选择，从而统一物理信息获取与用户意图澄清。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交互式消歧方法主要依赖向用户提问来获取额外信息，但具身环境中目标歧义不仅源于用户意图，还可能由当前观察中缺失的任务相关物理证据导致（如遮挡、受限视角、不可读文本、未观察到的目标），这要求机器人主动改变观察方式而非仅依赖提问。

Method: 提出一个以主动观察为骨干的具身目标消歧框架，使用视觉-语言模型根据累积的视觉证据和交互信息进行决策，决定是继续观察、请求澄清还是完成目标选择。主动观察既能直接恢复缺失的判别性证据，也能揭示物体名称、标签和语义属性，从而在仍有必要时改善用户澄清效果。

Result: 真实机器人实验表明，该框架能够在统一的具身消歧过程中融合物理信息获取与用户意图澄清。

Conclusion: 主动观察与用户澄清可以协同工作，在统一的具身消歧框架中互补，比单纯依赖提问的交互式方法更全面地解决目标歧义问题。

Abstract: Natural language provides robots with a flexible task interface, but target ambiguity in embodied environments arises not only from user intent; it can also result from missing taskrelevant physical evidence in the current observation. Existing interactive disambiguation methods primarily obtain additional information by asking the user, whereas occlusion, restricted viewpoints, unreadable text, and unobserved targets require the robot to actively change its observation. We propose an active-perception framework for embodied target disambiguation that uses active observation as the backbone for information acquisition and uses a vision-language model to decide, on the basis of accumulated visual evidence and interaction information, whether to continue observing, request clarification, or complete target selection. Active observation can both directly recover missing discriminative evidence and reveal object names, labels, and semantic attributes, thereby improving user clarification when it remains necessary. Real-robot experiments show that the framework combines physical information acquisition and userintent clarification within a unified embodied disambiguation process.

</details>


### [62] [MobileMem: Learning from a Year of Mobile Experiences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13606)
*Xinle Deng,Yida Xue,Xiangyuan Ru,Haoming Xu,Shuofei Qiao,Mengru Wang,Yijun Chen,Buqiang Xu,Chen Jiang,Yuchen Eleanor Jiang,Lizhong Wang,Jianfeng Wang,Li Zeng,Haofen Wang,Guilin Qi,Huajun Chen,Ningyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MobileMem是一个面向设备端长期记忆的基准测试与框架,基于一年尺度的移动设备使用经验数据,通过知识驱动的合成流水线构建连贯且时间一致的长时程轨迹,提供文本与多模态设置,覆盖多跳推理、时间推理、知识更新和隐式偏好推断等能力,推动AI助手从信息检索迈向体验式智能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 下一代AI助手需要从回答孤立问题转向持续性的个人助理,必须具备长期记忆来积累和利用用户的个性化经验。然而现有基准无法充分适配真实移动设备场景,因为该场景下的经验具有异构性、多模态性、时间演化性和深度个性化等特点。

Method: MobileMem采用知识驱动的合成流水线,从用户与应用会话中构建连贯且时间一致的长时间跨度轨迹;提供互补的文本和多模态设置,覆盖多跳推理、时间推理、知识更新和隐式偏好推断等任务;通过模拟经验而非孤立事实,使记忆超越信息检索范畴。

Result: MobileMem为智能体提供"记住过去、理解现在、适应未来"的能力评估,在真实移动设备环境中实现了持续个人学习,将记忆从信息检索提升为体验式智能。

Conclusion: MobileMem通过建模用户的完整体验而非孤立事实,为持续的个人学习提供了新的范式,代表了移动端长期记忆研究从信息检索向体验式智能的重要转变。

Abstract: The next generation of AI agents is increasingly moving beyond systems that answer isolated questions toward persistent personal assistants that can understand, remember, and continuously learn from users' experiences. Such assistants require long-term memory to accumulate and leverage user-specific experiences over time, yet existing benchmarks remain inadequate for realistic mobile settings, where experiences are heterogeneous, multimodal, evolving, and deeply personal. We introduce MobileMem, a benchmark and framework for studying on-device long-term memory, grounded in a year-scale collection of mobile experiences. MobileMem employs a knowledge-grounded synthesis pipeline to construct coherent and temporally consistent long-horizon trajectories from user-app sessions. It provides complementary text and multimodal settings covering multi-hop and temporal reasoning, knowledge updating, and implicit preference inference. Specifically, MobileMem enables agents to remember the past, understand the present, and adapt to the future. By modeling experiences rather than isolated facts, MobileMem moves memory beyond information retrieval toward experiential intelligence for continuous personal learning.

</details>


### [63] [No Universal Signal Predicts Sample-Level LLM Regression under Version Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13607)
*Jia Sheng,Yiwei Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究前沿大语言模型更新后个别样本出现回归（旧模型答对、新模型答错）的问题，比较了单模型信号（置信度、logit边际、注意力熵）与跨版本信号（输出KL散度、似然漂移、token级KL、表征漂移）在推断时预测回归的能力。在六个基准、三个任务族、六组模型更新对上的实验表明：信号有效性依赖任务类型和模型更新，但跨版本信号在置信度失效时仍能提供信息（甚至无需标签），支持了将高风险样本路由回旧模型的"选择性回退"方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型频繁更新，聚合指标虽提升但无法反映个别样本的回归现象（旧模型正确、新模型错误的响应）。目前缺乏系统性地比较不同推断时信号在预测这类回归方面的有效性，以及如何在不同任务和模型更新场景下选择合适信号的问题。

Method: 论文设计了一个统一的"增量价值测试"框架，在置信度基线之上隔离评估每种信号的独立增益。比较了四类单模型信号（置信度、logit边际、注意力熵）和四类跨版本信号（输出KL散度、似然漂移、token级KL、表征漂移），覆盖六个基准（MCQ、数学推理、代码生成三个任务族）和六组模型更新对，并验证了无标签情况下跨版本信号的可选择性，提出选择性回退方案将高风险样本路由回旧模型。

Result: 研究发现：(1) 信号有效性依赖任务类型——置信度在MCQ和简单数学问题上最强，而似然/KL信号在较难数学和代码生成上增益更频繁；(2) 没有一种信号在跨模型更新时普遍最优；(3) 部分跨版本信号在置信度失效时仍保持信息量，甚至在无标签条件下也能工作，可支持将高风险样本回退到旧模型的选择性回退方法。

Conclusion: 实践者可根据任务层级模式，针对给定的模型更新选择应信任的回归信号。跨版本信号为置信度失效的困难场景提供了补充方案，无标签条件下的有效性使得选择性回退成为可行的工程手段。

Abstract: Frontier LLMs are updated frequently and typically outperform their predecessors in aggregate. But aggregate gains say little about individual samples: an update can still cause sample-level regression, where a response correct under the old model becomes incorrect under the new one. This paper studies how to predict such regressions from signals available at inference time. We compare single-model signals (confidence, logit margin, attention entropy) against cross-version signals (output KL divergence, likelihood drift, token-level KL, representation drift) under a unified added-value test that isolates each signal's gain over a confidence baseline. Across six benchmarks in three task families (multiple-choice question answering, or MCQ; math reasoning; code generation) and six model update pairs, we find that (1) signal effectiveness is task-dependent: confidence is strongest on MCQ and simpler math, while likelihood/KL signals give the most frequent gains on harder math and code; (2) no signal is universally best across model updates either; and (3) some cross-version signals stay informative even when confidence fails, including without labels, which supports a proof-of-concept selective fallback that routes high-risk samples back to the old model. Practitioners can use these task-level patterns to choose which regression signal to trust for a given update. Code is available at https://github.com/jiashengsally/llm-regression-signals.

</details>


### [64] [Evaluating Agentic Learning Harness Capabilities Without Labels via the Scaling Hypothesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13608)
*Aryan Luthra,Kshitij Jain,Siddharth Arya,Bobby Filar,Anna Bertiger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需标注基准的框架，用于评估网络安全场景中的持续学习"智能体系统"（LLM+检索/记忆）。该方法基于缩放假说：用更强的教师模型对较小的学生模型提供稀疏校正，通过学生向教师收敛的程度来评分系统，并验证了"教师相对提升"可作为真实系统增益的有效代理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络安全中的持续学习系统通常用标注基准衡量，但基准标签稀缺、过时且不具代表性，从业者难以判断系统是否有效或比较两个系统的优劣。传统的LLM-as-a-judge因评估能力不高于被评估者而信号微弱，蒸馏在标签稀疏、偶发且有偏差时也不可靠。

Method: 提出一个无需标注基准的端到端评估框架，基于缩放假说：由更强的教师模型向带持续学习系统的较小学生模型提供稀疏采样校正，通过学生模型随时间向教师收敛的程度来为系统评分。

Result: 在多种安全任务、模型族和系统设计上，相对于教师的提升与相对于保留的金标准的提升相关，验证了教师相对提升作为标签缺失时真实系统增益代理的有效性。同时证明相同量级模型间的LLM-as-a-judge无法提供可用信号。

Conclusion: 结果表明，当人类提供同样类型的稀疏、高精度校正时，教师规模的大模型可以通过相同的持续学习系统得到改进，为无标签环境下的系统评估提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Agentic "Continual Learning Harnesses", systems that pair an LLM with retrieval or memory to improve from feedback without retraining, have shown growing value in cybersecurity. But their value is conventionally measured by gains against labeled benchmarks, an approach that often fails in operational security settings. Benchmark labels are scarce, stale, and unrepresentative, so a practitioner often cannot tell whether a given harness helps at all or which of two is better for their task. Traditional LLM-as-a-judge offers little signal because it is no stronger than the agent it evaluates, and distillation is unreliable on scarce, sporadic, and biased labels.
  We propose a framework for evaluating learning harnesses end-to-end without a labeled benchmark, grounded in the scaling hypothesis. A stronger teacher model provides sparsely sampled corrections to a smaller student with a continual learning harness. We score a harness by how much its student converges toward the teacher over time. Across security tasks, model families, and harness designs, we show that improvement relative to the teacher correlates with improvement relative to a held-out gold standard, validating teacher-relative lift as a proxy for true harness uplift when labels are absent. We further show that LLM-as-a-judge between similarly powered models yields no usable signal. These results suggest that a teacher-sized model can be improved through the same harness when humans provide the same kind of sparse, high-precision corrections.

</details>


### [65] [SemPlan: Benchmarking Structured Semantic Planning for LLM-Based Queries over Enterprise Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13612)
*Bruno Santos Teixeira*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SemPlan Benchmark是一个确定性合成双语基准，包含1,800个案例（英语和巴西葡萄牙语），用于评估企业数据自然语言接口的四种架构：直接SQL生成（A1）、有界工具智能体（A2）、结构化语义请求+确定性规划（A3）以及带澄清/有状态语义规划变体（A4）。结果显示A3正确率最高（25.67%），A1策略合规率最高且不安全/无效率最低，A4 API成本最低且虚假拒绝率最低，但所有架构绝对正确率均较低（22%-26%），支持权衡而非通用排序的解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业数据上的自然语言接口需要将未充分指定的请求转化为受治理、可执行的查询行为，同时控制无效查询、策略失败、成本和不确定性。现有研究缺乏对这类架构设计空间的系统评估，因此需要构建一个确定性基准来比较不同架构的优缺点。

Method: 构建了包含1,800个案例（1,200个冻结科学评估子集）的确定性合成双语基准（英语和巴西葡萄牙语），在相同模型配置下比较四种架构：A1（直接SQL生成）、A2（有界工具智能体）、A3（结构化语义请求生成+确定性规划执行）、A4（带澄清/有状态语义规划变体）。通过4,800条主记录进行正确性、策略合规性、成本等多维度评估。

Result: A3正确率最高（25.67%），显著优于A1、A2和A4；A1策略合规率最高且不安全/无效率最低；A4平均API成本最低且虚假拒绝率最低。在150例稳定性子集上，答案正确可重复性为92.00%-98.67%。但所有架构绝对正确率均较低（22.25%-25.67%）。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持权衡解释而非通用排序：额外的结构约束改变了失败模式和效率，但未单调提升正确性，也未解决歧义和多轮状态一致性问题。这表明在自然语言到企业数据接口中，不同架构各有优劣，应根据具体需求选择。

Abstract: Natural-language interfaces to enterprise data must translate underspecified requests into governed, executable behavior while controlling invalid queries, policy failures, cost, and nondeterminism. SemPlan Benchmark evaluates this architectural design space with a deterministic synthetic bilingual benchmark containing 1,800 cases in English and Brazilian Portuguese; 1,200 cases form the frozen scientific evaluation subset. Four architectures are compared under the same model configuration: direct SQL generation (A1), a bounded tool-agent baseline (A2), structured semantic-request generation followed by deterministic planning and execution (A3), and a clarification/stateful semantic-plan variant (A4). Across 4,800 primary records, answer correctness was low in absolute terms: 22.25% for A1, 22.58% for A2, 25.67% for A3, and 24.25% for A4. A3 had the highest observed correctness and significantly exceeded A1, A2, and A4 in the pre-specified paired correctness analysis, while A1 retained the highest policy-correct rate and the lowest unsafe-or-invalid rate. A4 had the lowest mean API cost and lowest false-refusal rate. On a preselected 150-case stability subset, answer-correct repeatability ranged from 92.00% to 98.67%. The results support a trade-off interpretation rather than a universal ranking: additional structural constraints changed failure modes and efficiency, but did not monotonically improve correctness or solve ambiguity and multi-turn state consistency.

</details>


### [66] [How Compliant is Sepsis Treatment? An Expert-Guided Neuro-symbolic Pipeline for Generating Clinical Compliance Insights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13617)
*Himanshu Tripathi,Kaushik Roy,Subash Neupane,Shahram Rahimi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种专家引导的神经符号管道，用于验证脓毒症临床护理是否符合循证方案。该方法将大语言模型严格约束为语义归一化任务，将杂乱的药物和微生物字符串映射到固定临床词汇表，再由Sugeno模糊推理系统对归一化事件进行推理，编码八条Surviving Sepsis Campaign捆绑规则并以[0,1]连续评分替代二元判断。在MIMIC-IV v3.1的2,438例脓毒症病例上验证，发现抗生素时机是最关键的缺陷环节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证临床护理是否符合循证方案本质上是神经符号问题，但在安全关键场景中，纯神经或纯符号范式都无法独立胜任——需要结合两者的优势。

Method: 采用专家引导的两阶段管道：(1) 大语言模型被严格约束仅做语义归一化，将混乱的临床文本映射到固定词汇；(2) Sugeno模糊推理系统对归一化事件进行推理，编码八条脓毒症集束化规则并以分级评分替代二元判断。

Result: 在2,438例MIMIC-IV v3.1脓毒症病例中，发现抗生素时机是最关键的缺陷（均值0.24，仅13%在一小时内），第1小时集束依从性不足（均值36.7%），51%的乳酸升高病例出现下降缺失，依从性差/好的ICU住院天数存在差异（3.8天 vs 5.1天）。

Conclusion: 神经符号混合架构能有效完成循证护理依从性验证，模糊推理提供的分级评分比二元判定更能揭示各环节的精细缺陷，具有临床应用潜力。

Abstract: Verifying whether clinical care follows evidence-based protocols is a natural neuro-symbolic problem, yet the safety-critical setting defeats either paradigm alone. We present an expert-guided pipeline that constrains a large language model strictly to semantic normalization, mapping messy drug and microbiology strings onto a fixed clinical vocabulary, while a Sugeno fuzzy inference system reasons over the normalized events. The fuzzy layer encodes eight Surviving Sepsis Campaign bundle rules and replaces binary judgments with graded scores in [0,1]. Applied to 2,438 MIMIC-IV v3.1 sepsis episodes, it surfaces antibiotic timing as the most critical breakdown (mean 0.24, 13% within one hour), Hour-1 underperformance (mean 36.7%), a 51% elevated-lactate drop-off, and descriptive differences in ICU stay across compliance groups (3.8 versus 5.1 days).

</details>


### [67] [Algorithm Design and Physician Liability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13618)
*Shujie Luan,Shubhranshu Singh,Tinglong Dai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究AI医疗决策中责任规则如何影响算法设计与医师使用行为。责任规则可能导致AI在不同患者群体间的差异化使用，且该效应是非单调的；强制算法在群体间精度一致反而可能损害两个群体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI在临床决策中广泛应用，但同一算法在不同患者群体间可能产生不等的准确性，引发公平性担忧。美国引入责任规则，要求医疗服务提供者为依赖不平等算法导致的错误临床决策负责。作者希望理解这种责任考量如何重塑AI公司的算法设计决策（决定群体间精度差异）和医师的AI使用决策。

Method: 构建一个AI公司与医师的博弈模型。AI公司为两个患者群体设计算法，提升弱势群体精度的成本更高；医师作为最终负责任决策者，权衡降低临床不确定性与预期的责任暴露（当AI错误不成比例地影响弱势群体时），决定是否咨询AI。

Result: 研究发现责任规则可诱导AI的差异化使用：医师可能总体上减少AI使用，且在中间责任范围内对弱势群体使用AI更少。效应是非单调的——随责任增加，医师对弱势群体的AI使用先下降后上升，原因是公司重新分配投资以缩小差距或转向等精度设计。强制群体间算法精度一致反而可能无意中损害两个群体，因为统一精度要求扭曲了公司的投资激励和医师的均衡AI使用决策。

Conclusion: 责任规则虽旨在抑制算法歧视，但其对AI使用的影响是复杂且非单调的。监管者需谨慎对待简单的"统一精度"强制要求，因为这可能产生适得其反的效果，损害所有患者群体的利益。

Abstract: A single clinical algorithm can deliver unequal accuracy across patient groups, and concern about such disparity has grown as artificial intelligence (AI) spreads through clinical decision-making. In response, a liability rule introduced in the United States holds healthcare providers responsible when their reliance on disparate algorithms contributes to erroneous clinical decisions. We examine how such liability considerations reshape (i) an AI firm's algorithm design decisions that drive group-specific accuracy and (ii) a physician's decisions to use AI in healthcare delivery. The AI firm designs an algorithm for two patient groups, and improving accuracy for the disadvantaged group is more costly. The physician (who remains the accountable decision-maker) then decides whether to consult AI, weighing the reduction in clinical uncertainty against expected liability exposure when AI errors disproportionately affect the disadvantaged group. We find the liability rule can induce disparate use of AI: the physician may reduce AI use overall and, over an intermediate range of liability, rely on AI less for disadvantaged patients. The effect is non-monotone. As liability increases, the physician's use of AI for disadvantaged patients first declines, then rises as the firm reallocates investment toward reducing disparity or switches to an equal-accuracy design. Mandating equal algorithmic accuracy across patient groups can then inadvertently harm both groups, because a uniform accuracy requirement distorts the firm's investment incentives and the physician's equilibrium AI-use decisions.

</details>


### [68] [Your Probabilistic JEPA Is Secretly a Hidden Markov Model: A State-Space Interpretation of Joint-Embedding Predictive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13621)
*Yongchao Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文证明时间索引的预测信息瓶颈JEPA（PIB-VJEPA）与隐马尔可夫模型（HMM）具有相同的计算结构：随机上下文编码器对应滤波分布，概率预测器对应隐状态动力学，解码器/逆目标编码器对应发射方向。作者区分了4级对应强度并给出了精确序列级HMM等价的充分条件，同时提出了马尔可夫链JEPA（MCJEPA）作为具体实例，其矩阵幂保证多时间尺度Chapman-Kolmogorov一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为时间JEPA框架提供有原则的状态空间解释，将其与经典HMM理论建立严格联系，从而揭示JEPA各组件（编码器、预测器、解码器）在概率建模中的角色，并指导可证明一致性的模型设计。

Method: 1) 建立PIB-VJEPA与HMM三要素（滤波/转移/发射）的结构映射；2) 定义4级由弱到强的对应层级并给出精确等价的充分条件；3) 提出MCJEPA，用学习到的转移矩阵替代潜在预测器，在有限时间齐次情形下用矩阵幂保证Chapman-Kolmogorov一致性；4) 扩展到条件离散状态转移、连续状态马尔可夫核、连续时间动力学；5) 将确定性时间JEPA解释为退化的Dirac核特例；6) 将预测信息瓶颈解释为压缩促最小化、残差可预测性测充分性；7) 通过受控实验验证转移复合、滤波解释与预测马尔可夫化。

Result: 受控实验支持转移组成、滤波解释、已知合成过程中的预测马尔可夫化，以及JEPA潜在预测与HMM式序列学习之间的区别，验证了理论框架的正确性。

Conclusion: 时间JEPA获得了一个有原则的状态空间解释：其本质上是HMM结构的现代摊销化实现，这为JEPA类模型的理论分析、可证明的一致性保证以及更深入的设计提供了基础。

Abstract: A hidden Markov model (HMM) combines three roles: inference of a hidden-state belief from observations, propagation through a Markov transition, and emission back to observation space. We show that full, time-indexed Predictive Information Bottleneck VJEPA (PIB-VJEPA) exposes the same computational structure: a stochastic context encoder plays the role of an amortized filtering distribution, a probabilistic predictor defines latent-state dynamics, and a decoder, inverse target encoder, or induced implicit conditional supplies the emission direction. We distinguish 4 progressively stronger levels of correspondence and give sufficient conditions for exact sequence-level HMM equivalence. To make the connection concrete, we introduce Markov-Chain JEPA (MCJEPA), which replaces the latent predictor by a learned transition matrix; in the finite time-homogeneous case, matrix powers guarantee exact multi-horizon Chapman--Kolmogorov consistency. Conditioned discrete-state transitions, continuous-state Markov kernels, and continuous-time dynamics extend this construction, while deterministic temporal JEPA appears as a degenerate Dirac-kernel special case. We further interpret predictive information-bottleneck learning as seeking a compact predictive state: compression promotes minimality, while residual predictability tests sufficiency. Controlled experiments support transition composition, the filtering interpretation, predictive Markovization in a known synthetic process, and the distinction between JEPA latent prediction and HMM-style sequence learning. Together, these results give temporal JEPA a principled state-space interpretation.

</details>


### [69] [ARC: Fair Relative Advantage Comparison in Open-Ended Real-World Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13622)
*Yongqi Tong,Tan Li Hui Faith,Choy Zhen Wen Marcus,Zhou Jin,Kewei Fu,Jiang-Ming Yang,Jianshe Li,Xin Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ARC（Advantage Regularization via Conditioning）训练方法，解决开放域交互中基于组的强化学习因行为多样性导致的奖励公平性问题，并提出Interacting范式实现响应式、可操纵的智能体交互，构建了策略标注的训练语料Inter-86K。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放域真实交互中存在多种合理行为（直接回答、询问澄清、进度更新、行动前确认等），这破坏了基于组的RL核心假设——组内rollout不再保证行为可比较。奖励模型对交互风格的偏好会扭曲相对优势，使优化偏向奖励偏好行为而非上下文合适行为，形成奖励公平性问题。

Method: 提出ARC训练方案：通过策略条件化rollout分组恢复更公平的相对比较，结合混合奖励和熵正则化。同时提出Interacting范式，将用户可见的交流与潜在推理和工具使用解耦，并提供标注与蒸馏管线构建Inter-86K训练语料。

Result: ARC显著增强核心tau/tau²工具使用基准性能；Interacting将time-to-first-token从4.91秒降至1.27秒（相对think风格基线）。代码和数据将公开。

Conclusion: 开放端交互学习的关键瓶颈不仅在于智能体如何被奖励，更在于其行为是否首先被公平比较。核心贡献在于揭示奖励公平性这一被忽视的问题，并提供ARC与Interacting两个互补的解决方案。

Abstract: Open-ended real-world interaction admits multiple valid behaviors: an agent may answer directly, ask for clarification, provide progress updates, or confirm before acting. This flexibility breaks a core assumption behind group-based RL: rollouts compared within a group are no longer guaranteed to be behaviorally comparable. As a result, reward-model preferences over interaction style can distort relative advantages and steer optimization toward reward-preferred behaviors rather than context-appropriate ones. We formalize this as a \textit{reward fairness problem} and propose \textbf{ARC} (Advantage Regularization via Conditioning), a training recipe that restores fairer relative comparison through strategy-conditioned rollout grouping, together with hybrid rewards and entropy regularization. We study ARC in our proposed \inter, a novel paradigm for responsive, steerable, and execution-aware user-agent interaction that decouples user-visible communication from latent reasoning and tool use. \inter\ also provides the annotation and distillation pipeline for constructing \inter-86K, our strategy-annotated training corpus for supervised and RL training. Empirically, ARC substantially strengthens the core $τ/τ^2$ tool-use benchmarks, while \inter\ reduces time-to-first-token from 4.91s to 1.27s relative to a think-style baseline. Together, these results suggest that a central bottleneck in open-ended interactive learning is not only how agents are rewarded, but whether their behaviors are compared fairly in the first place. The ARC implementation and \inter-86K training data will be released.

</details>


### [70] [Reward Machines for Signal Temporal Logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13625)
*Alper Kamil Bozkurt,Shangtong Zhang,Yuichi Motai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于自动机的STL控制综合方法，通过构建时间交替自动机为强化学习提供高效的记忆机制和马尔可夫奖励，解决了STL鲁棒性依赖执行历史导致状态空间爆炸的问题，实验证明该方法在鲁棒性得分和满足率上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: STL鲁棒性得分依赖执行历史，对于具有任意嵌套时间算子的长期规范会导致状态空间爆炸，使得基于鲁棒性奖励的强化学习方法不可扩展。同时，现代自主系统和AI系统缺乏精确完整的系统模型，需要学习型控制方法。

Method: 从给定的STL规范构建时间交替自动机，用自动机位置和时钟估值增广状态空间，并从自动机接受条件推导出马尔可夫奖励，为RL框架提供高效的记忆机制。

Result: 实验表明，该方法学习的策略在鲁棒性得分和满意度上均优于使用鲁棒性奖励的现有方法。

Conclusion: 自动机驱动的记忆机制和马尔可夫奖励有效解决了STL规范下RL状态空间爆炸问题，提升了控制策略学习的性能和规范满足率。

Abstract: Signal temporal logic (STL) provides a formal language for specifying real-time properties of real-valued observations, along with a quantitative robustness score for monitoring satisfaction. Control synthesis from STL specifications is of interest since manual controller design becomes infeasible as real-world systems grow in complexity. Moreover, many modern autonomous and AI-enabled systems lack accurate and complete system models, which makes optimization-based synthesis approaches unsuitable and motivates learning-based control. Prior work uses STL robustness scores as rewards in reinforcement learning (RL) to obtain control policies satisfying given specifications; however, robustness depends on execution history, leading to intractable state space expansion for general long-horizon specifications with arbitrarily nested temporal operators. This work introduces a novel automata-based approach that provides an efficient memory mechanism and associated Markovian rewards suitable for RL frameworks. Our approach constructs a timed alternating automaton from the given STL specifications, augments the state space with automaton locations and clock valuations, and derives rewards from the automaton acceptance condition. We empirically demonstrate that our approach learns policies that achieve higher robustness scores and satisfaction rates than those learned by existing approaches using robustness-based rewards.

</details>


### [71] [A Calibrated Test of Internal Action Maps: State Signals Without Global Affine Closure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13626)
*Dekun Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过证据格(lattice)系统测试了隐藏状态信号的可解码性、因果可用性与可复用动作图之间的可分离性。在仿射S_5载体、后训练的Qwen/Qwen3-4B模型及学习到的有限世界上验证：状态可用性、因果使用、局部几何与可复用闭合是彼此独立的属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究往往假设隐藏状态可解码或因果可用就必然支持可复用的动作图(即映射在不同源/域间能自然组合)。作者质疑这一隐含假设，试图证明这些性质是可分离的，从而为模型内部表征的可靠校准提供边界而非普适结论。

Method: 设计了证据格(lattice)测试框架，包含一步、组合、逆映射、解码和交换性五类门控(gate)测试；在三个层面上验证：(1)几何分支上有已知仿射S_5载体；(2)后训练Qwen/Qwen3-4B模型的冻结末token h28层仿射映射，含七种随机实体划分和多层(h4/h16/h28/h36)比较；(3)学习到的有限世界。结合结构化曲率、持域共轭、干预数据集与结果感知重拟合来交叉验证。

Result: 所有持有源折痕在S_5载体上通过五类门控；曲率和共轭单调提高误差，但30个最强单元中仅23个翻转闭合门。在Qwen中，h28冻结仿射映射的持实体误差0.519(测试域内交叉拟合为0.398)，多层对比显示h4/h16更优的一步拟合但h4冲突状态解码弱。结果感知重拟合改善h28一步误差至0.474，但无一重拟合通过组合门。因果效应仅在h28/h36出现。

Conclusion: 在测试的载体范围内，状态可用性、因果使用、局部几何与可复用闭合是可分离的独立属性——可解码或因果可用并不意味着支持可复用的动作图。这一结论受限于单一预训练模型、采样到的末token层、两个有限世界及测试的仿射/诊断函数类。

Abstract: A hidden state signal can be decodable or causally usable without supporting a reusable action map. We test whether action maps fitted without a source reach its natural post-action activation and compose. We organize the tests as an evidence lattice and validate the geometric branch on a known affine S_5 carrier: all held-source folds pass one-step, composition, inverse, decoding, and commutativity gates. Structured curvature and held-domain conjugacy raise error monotonically, but only 23/30 strongest cells flip a closure gate, bounding rather than universalizing calibration. In post-trained Qwen/Qwen3-4B, frozen final-token h28 affine maps have mean held-entity error .519, versus .398 for within-test-domain cross-fit. Seven randomized entity splits and map geometry do not support a purely entity-specific account. Earlier h4/h16 layers fit one-step transitions better, but h4 conflict-state decoding is weak and lexical controls remain unresolved. Three matched intervention datasets regenerated from one frozen checkpoint show causal effects only at h28/h36. Outcome-aware refitting improves h28 one-step error to .474 (.469 with weighting), yet no refit passes composition. Learned finite worlds likewise preserve relative algebraic signals or shared charts without held-source affine closure. Within the tested carriers, state availability, causal use, local geometry, and reusable closure are separable. The result is limited to one pretrained model, sampled final-token layers, two finite worlds, and the tested affine or diagnostic function classes.

</details>


### [72] [Exploring ESC Winners with Nested Diagrams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13630)
*Anurag Sharma,Marcel Nöhre,Gerd Stumme*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了ConceptFlow，一个兼容scikit-learn的Python库，用于形式概念分析，可构建和渲染多值形式背景的嵌套线图。作者将其应用于1975-2025年欧洲歌唱大赛获胜者，探索投票模式与音乐特征之间的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形式概念分析（FCA）在处理多值形式背景时缺乏易于使用的工具，特别是缺少支持概念标尺、嵌套线图可视化的scikit-learn兼容库，这限制了FCA在实际数据分析中的应用。

Method: ConceptFlow库实现概念标尺化（conceptual scaling），计算因子格（factor lattices），识别子直积中的填充节点，并生成交互式可视化。在案例研究中，外层标尺覆盖投票支持（区域、文化、历史、政治维度），内层标尺覆盖音乐特征（节奏和调性），构建嵌套线图。

Result: 生成的嵌套线图揭示了两层标尺之间的蕴含关系，展示了获胜歌曲的投票方式与其共享音乐特征之间的依赖关系。

Conclusion: ConceptFlow为FCA提供了实用的Python工具，并通过欧洲歌唱大赛案例展示了嵌套线图在多维数据分析中的解释能力。

Abstract: We present ConceptFlow, a scikit-learn-compatible Python library for Formal Concept Analysis that constructs and renders nested line diagrams from many-valued formal contexts. Given a many-valued context and a partition of its attributes into conceptual scales, ConceptFlow performs conceptual scaling, computes the factor lattices, identifies filled nodes of the corresponding subdirect product, and produces an interactive visualization.
  We apply ConceptFlow to the winners of the Eurovision Song Contest from 1975 to 2025, exploring relationships between voting patterns and musical characteristics. Voting support is captured by an outer scale spanning regional, cultural, historical, and political dimensions, while an inner scale captures musical characteristics via tempo and key. The resulting nested line diagram reveals implications across both scales, exposing dependencies between how winning entries were voted for and the musical properties they share.

</details>


### [73] [Ontology-Grounded Project Memory for Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13662)
*James Adam*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MOOSEDev是一个为编码代理提供基于本体论结构化项目记忆的系统，通过知识图谱和MCP接口向代理暴露架构决策、经验教训、约束和理由。相比生产级向量记忆工具，MOOSEDev在supersession、集合完整性、否定问题等任务上准确率高达0.98-1.00，而基线仅6%-27%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码代理已成为许多软件项目的主要代码生成方式，其带来的变更速度使得追踪变更背后的原因变得困难。现有的向量记忆工具在检索结构化、带有生命周期状态和溯源信息的记录时表现不佳。

Method: MOOSEDev通过知识图谱捕获架构决策、经验教训、约束和理由，每条记录携带生命周期状态、溯源信息和取代链接。系统通过MCP接口向代理暴露记忆，并使用MOOSE神经符号引擎，将符号层作为主要推理基底。

Result: 在835条中性公共语料上对比，MOOSEDev在supersession、集合完整性和否定问题上返回准确率0.98-1.00，而基线top-k检索仅6%-27%。相关性和token成本两者大致相当。

Conclusion: 基于本体论的结构化符号记忆在需要精确推理（如取代关系、集合完整性、否定）的任务上远优于向量检索，同时不牺牲相关性和token效率。作者还提供了时间提交历史引导、预注册实时试验和经验教训。

Abstract: Coding agents have become the primary means of generating new code in many software projects, and the resulting velocity of changes makes keeping track of the reasons behind those changes challenging. This paper introduces MOOSEDev, a system designed to give coding agents structured, ontology-grounded project memory. The system captures architectural decisions, lessons, constraints, and rationales in a knowledge graph exposed to agents via a Model Context Protocol (MCP) interface. Records carry lifecycle status, provenance, and supersession links, queryable via MOOSE, a proprietary neurosymbolic engine that treats the symbolic layer as the primary reasoning substrate. We compared MOOSEDev against a production vector-memory tool on a neutral public corpus of 835 typed records. MOOSEDev returned the expected answer set essentially in full (0.98-1.00) on supersession, set-completeness, and negation questions, whereas the baseline's top-k retrieval surfaced between 6% and 27%. Conversely, relevance recall and token cost were largely equivalent between the two systems. We also describe a temporal commit-history bootstrap of our own codebase, a pre-registered live trial, and lessons learned.

</details>


### [74] [Second Thought: Reasoning in Parallel as LLM Agents Act and Observe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13667)
*Zhensu Sun,Chengran Yang,Yunbo Lyu,Jieke Shi,David Lo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Second Thought框架，一种无需训练的推理框架，在ReAct智能体的Thought阶段结束后并行派生辅助分支进行推理，利用动作执行与观察等待的"推理空闲窗口"进行并行思考，从而在不增加主线程顺序解码负担的情况下提升智能体性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ReAct范式的LLM智能体中，深思熟虑的推理仅局限于Thought阶段，而在Action执行和Observation等待期间推理处于冻结状态，形成"推理空闲窗口"，这未被利用，存在提升空间。

Method: Second Thought是训练无关的推断框架：在每个Thought阶段结束后派生四个辅助分支，与主循环并发解码，当环境观察结果返回时将这些并行生成的思考合并回主线程。这样将额外推理从主线程的顺序解码路径上移出。

Result: 在三个智能体基准和三个推理LLM上，Second Thought在所有九个(model, benchmark)组合中降低了平均回合数，在六个组合中将主线程解码减少高达43%（平均约20%），一个组合基本不变；Pass@1在九个组合中七个无显著变化，两个显著差异为+12.4和+10.2分。与计算匹配的对照组相比，在所有四个适用设置中实现更高的Pass@1且顺序解码减少1.3到3.2倍。

Conclusion: 通过利用推理空闲窗口进行并行思考，Second Thought能够在不牺牲甚至提升任务性能的同时减少主线程的顺序解码开销，为ReAct式智能体的推理效率提供了新的优化方向。

Abstract: LLM agents in the ReAct paradigm alternate between reasoning, acting, and observing, but deliberate reasoning is confined to the Thought phase: while the agent serializes an action and waits for the environment, its reasoning is frozen. We identify this recurring interval for Action and Observation as a reasoning idle window and ask whether it can host additional reasoning in parallel that serves future turns. Therefore, we propose Second Thought, a training-free inference framework that forks four auxiliary branches the instant each Thought phase concludes, decodes them concurrently with the main loop, and merges the generated thoughts back when the environment observation arrives. In this way, Second Thought relocates the added reasoning off the main thread's sequential decoding path. Across three agentic benchmarks and three reasoning LLMs, Second Thought lowers the average turn count in all nine (model,benchmark) pairs and reduces main thread decoding in six of them by up to 43% (roughly 20% on average among those settings), while leaving it essentially unchanged in a seventh; Pass@1 shows no significant change in seven of nine pairs and the two significant differences are +12.4 and +10.2 points. Against a compute-matched control that forces an equivalent budget onto the main thread's own reasoning, it attains strictly higher Pass@1 with 1.3 to 3.2 less sequential decoding in all four settings where the control applies.

</details>


### [75] [Learning to Assemble Novel Structures with Unfamiliar Parts under Semantic Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13684)
*Jonghyuk Park,Alex Lascarides,Subramanian Ramamoorthy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种神经符号架构，用于在具身对话和任务演示中学习组装新结构。核心场景是：代理在部署后遇到训练时未见过的语义约束（关于哪些部件类型和特征构成有效结构），初始并不知道相关结构和部件概念，需要通过用户交互逐步获取并利用这些知识。实验在模拟玩具卡车组装域中进行，从自然语言编码的符号证据和密集视觉观测中学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中的组装任务往往需要在部署后面对训练时未知的约束条件，代理不能预先掌握所有结构和部件的语义知识。因此需要一种能够在在线交互过程中，通过自然语言和演示不断获取新知识并进行结构调整的能力。

Method: 采用神经符号（neurosymbolic）架构，融合两类证据：(1) 以自然语言编码的符号证据（如 "dump trucks have a dumper"），传达语义约束；(2) 密集视觉观测的任务演示。代理在尝试组装过程中通过与用户交互来获取和利用这些知识。

Result: 实验表明，通过自然语言传达语义约束比仅依赖任务演示和/或仅通过自然语言命名部件，能实现更高效的数据（样本）在线适应。

Conclusion: 自然语言传递的语义约束对于组装任务的在线适应具有显著优势，神经符号方法能够有效整合符号知识和视觉观测，使代理在后部署阶段灵活学习新结构。

Abstract: This paper describes a neurosymbolic architecture for learning to assemble novel structures using evidence from embodied conversations and task demonstrations. We focus on scenarios where an agent encounters, after deployment, semantic constraints on structures--in other words, constraints as to which part types and features make valid structures--that were not available during training, and where it is initially unaware of the relevant structure and component part concepts. The agent must acquire and exploit such knowledge through user interactions while attempting assembly. We study this setting in a simulated toy truck assembly domain, learning from symbolic evidence encoded in natural language and from dense visual observations. Our experiments show that communicating semantic constraints through natural language (e.g., "dump trucks have a dumper") yields more data-efficient online adaptation than relying only on task demonstrations and/or only naming the parts through natural language.

</details>


### [76] [Coverage Aware Active Evaluation for Failure Discovery with Paired Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13719)
*Anjali Parashar,Rachel Luo,Apoorva Sharma,Sushant Veer,Edward Schmerling,Carson Sobolewski,Mingxin Yu,Chuchu Fan,Marco Pavone*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种自适应故障发现方法，通过控制变量残差建模校正代理（proxy）系统的故障信号来预测目标系统风险，并结合支持感知的互信息目标选择场景，在自动驾驶、操作和四足速度跟踪任务中发现比随机采样和主动学习基线多达2倍的故障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主系统可能以罕见且异构的方式失败，在有限测试预算下难以发现真实世界故障。尽管模拟器、低保真系统等相关代理可广泛采样，但由于sim-to-real和系统间差异，代理故障往往不能转移到真实世界。因此关键挑战是如何有效利用代理系统信息来准确预测目标系统的严重故障。

Method: 提出一种自适应故障发现方法：首先结合代理评估与有限的目标系统结果来引导目标系统测试的场景选择；其次通过控制变量启发（control-variate-inspired）的残差建模学习目标风险的局部预测器，对代理故障信号进行校正；最后将该预测器与支持感知的互信息目标相结合，偏向现实且充分支撑的区域，同时扩展故障模式的覆盖。

Result: 在自动驾驶、操作和四足速度跟踪三类任务中，该方法发现的故障数量是随机采样和主动学习基线的2倍，包括其他竞争方法未能发现的严重且多样的故障。

Conclusion: 通过融合代理系统信息与有限目标系统数据，配合残差校正和支持感知的多样性目标，可以有效提升目标系统故障发现的效率和覆盖率。

Abstract: Autonomous systems can fail in rare and heterogeneous ways, making real-world failure discovery difficult under limited testing budgets. Although cheaper proxies such as simulators, lower-fidelity systems, or related policies can be sampled extensively to find failures, proxy failures often do not transfer to the real world due to sim-to-real and system-to-system gaps. The key challenge is therefore to effectively leverage proxy system information for accurate prediction of severe target system failures. We propose an adaptive failure discovery method that combines proxy evaluations with limited target system results to guide scenario selection for target system testing. Our method learns a local predictor of target risk by correcting proxy failure signals using control-variate-inspired residual modeling. To find failures that are both likely and diverse, we combine this predictor with a support-aware mutual-information objective that favors realistic, well-supported regions while expanding coverage across failure modes. Across autonomous driving, manipulation, and quadruped velocity-tracking tasks, our method discovers up to 2$\times$ as many failures as random sampling and active-learning baselines, including severe and diverse failures missed by competing methods.

</details>


### [77] [Explanation Multiplicity: Circuit-Level Interpretability Evidence Does Not Survive Defensible Analytic Variation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13754)
*Ajay Pravin Mahale*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究检验欧盟AI法案下，机械可解释性（电路发现）产出的技术文档证据在不同分析人员采用不同合理设置时是否稳定。通过对GPT-2 small和间接宾语识别任务的15,840个预注册规格分析，发现73.2%的规格对产生翻转的声明，且底层电路结构近乎不重叠（Jaccard重叠中位数4%），功能不相关（Cohen's kappa 0.015），说明证据无法满足合规评估的可归档性标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欧盟AI法案要求高风险系统提供商提交技术文档，说明系统如何做出决策。机械可解释性（尤其是电路发现）是这类证据的天然来源，但在实际合规场景中，两个能力相当的分析人员使用相同工具和系统，采用不同但合理的设置时，产出的证据是否仍然一致？这一问题尚无实证研究回答。

Method: 作者预注册了一个跨越七个分析轴的交叉网格（每个水平取自已发表的实现），通过确定性声明映射将每个发现的电路映射为结构化的Annex IV声明。在GPT-2 small和间接宾语识别任务上运行15,840个预注册规格，其中7,561个产出了声明。进一步通过控制单一最影响变量（评估指标）、移除电路大小等方式进行敏感性分析。

Result: 73.2%的规格对发生声明翻转（95% CI 0.725-0.738），众数声明仅占41.1%的空间。标准化评估指标后翻转率仍为59.4%；移除电路大小并固定后为27.1%（高于预注册阈值）。电路间Jaccard重叠中位数仅4%，Cohen's kappa为0.015，表明不稳定性不是同一机制的不同描述。此外，七个记录在案的发现目标中有一个在该库自己的标准任务上完全无法执行。

Conclusion: 当前电路发现方法产出的证据无法满足合规评估机构可能接受的任何合理容差下的可归档性标准。作者提供了一个独立协议来评估可归档性，但强调研究仅覆盖一个模型和一个任务，结论在更大规模上是否成立尚待验证。

Abstract: The EU AI Act requires providers of high-risk systems to file technical documentation describing how the system reaches its decisions. Mechanistic interpretability is the obvious source of such evidence, and circuit discovery is its most developed instrument. We ask whether that evidence survives the condition under which it would be relied upon: two competent analysts, the same system, the same tool, different defensible settings.
  We pre-registered a crossed grid of seven analytic axes, every level taken from a published implementation, and mapped each discovered circuit through a deterministic claim map to a structured Annex IV statement. Across 15,840 pre-registered specifications on GPT-2 small and the indirect object identification task, of which 7,561 produced a claim, the derived statement flips across 73.2% of specification pairs (95% CI 0.725 to 0.738) and the modal claim commands 41.1% of the space. The evidence fails a filability criterion at every tolerance a conformity assessment body would plausibly accept.
  Standardising the single most influential choice, the evaluation metric, leaves the flip rate at 59.4%. Removing circuit size from the claim entirely and holding it fixed leaves 27.1% (95% CI 0.255 to 0.286), still above the pre-registered threshold. The circuits underlying these claims are structurally near-disjoint, median pairwise Jaccard overlap 4%, and functionally uncorrelated at Cohen's kappa 0.015, so the instability is not one mechanism described in different words.
  We give the filability criterion as a standalone protocol, and we report that one of the seven documented discovery objectives does not execute at all on the library's own canonical task. The study covers one model and one task, and whether the conclusion holds at scale is untested.

</details>


### [78] [Simulation-Aware In-Context Policy Improvement for LLM-Aided Analog Layout Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13767)
*Bingyang Liu,Ziming Wei,Xiaohan Gao,David Z. Pan*
