Abstract: We develop a method for estimating and testing a single block of a macroeconomic model with heterogeneous agents, without placing assumptions on the structure of the rest of the economy. In a large class of models, individual agents' decisions depend on the macroeconomy only through their expectations of the evolution of a finite-dimensional vector of "sufficient statistics" (e.g., asset returns or aggregate earnings). Our estimator selects the structural parameters that provide the best model-consistent fit between empirical impulse responses with respect to identified macro shocks of (a) cross-sectional moments of agent choices (e.g., moments of consumption) and (b) the vector of sufficient statistics. In a simulation illustration, we estimate a two-asset heterogeneous household model block without restricting production, firm investment, financial intermediation, monetary policy, trade, etc.

</details>


### [217] [Macroprudential Policy and Downside Risk: Regime-Dependent Effects of Capital Regulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14307)
*Vivien Czofa,Tibor Szendrei,Katalin Varga*

Main category: econ.EM

TL;DR: 本文使用阈值贝叶斯向量自回归（TBVAR）模型，以系统性压力指数（FISS）为阈值变量，估计匈牙利资本监管在不同状态（正常与压力）下的宏观经济效益。研究发现资本监管存在显著不对称性：压力期释放监管资本可在峰值显著提升GDP增长且效应持续约20个月，而正常期累积资本的代价在经济上可忽略不计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为数据受限的经济体提供一种实用的多变量替代方法来评估资本监管的宏观影响，该研究试图为逆周期资本缓冲的操作提供直接的经验证据，弥补Growth-at-Risk方法在数据约束下难以应用的不足。

Method: 采用阈值贝叶斯向量自回归（TBVAR）模型，以因子系统性压力指数（FISS）作为阈值变量，识别正常与压力两种状态（与偶发约束文献一致），并通过广义脉冲响应函数分析资本监管的宏观效应。

Result: 压力期释放监管资本在峰值显著提升GDP增长，效应持续约20个月；正常期累积资本的经济成本可忽略不计。结果对Cholesky排序、样本期和信贷变量定义的替代设定均保持稳健。

Conclusion: 研究结果直接支持了资本缓冲的逆周期操作：监管当局应在压力期释放资本以支持经济增长，而正常期累积资本的成本低廉，从而验证了逆周期资本缓冲的合理性。

Abstract: This paper employs a Threshold Bayesian Vector Autoregression (TBVAR) to estimate the regime-dependent macroeconomic effects of capital regulation in Hungary. Using the Factor-based Index of Systemic Stress (FISS) as the threshold variable, the model identifies normal and stress regimes consistent with the occasionally binding constraints literature. The TBVAR offers a practical multivariate alternative to Growth-at-Risk for data-constrained economies. Generalised impulse responses reveal a pronounced asymmetry: releasing regulatory capital during stress raises GDP growth at the peak, with effects persisting for roughly twenty months, while the cost of accumulating capital in the normal regime is economically negligible. These findings are robust to alternative Cholesky orderings, sample periods, and credit variable definitions, providing direct empirical support for the countercyclical operation of the capital buffer.

</details>


<div id='cs.CY'></div>

# cs.CY [[Back]](#toc)

### [218] [Asymmetric Discourse Homogenization and Shared Language Technology: Evidence from Reddit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13674)
*Fengming Liu*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文利用2019-2025年间两个跨党派论坛的600万条Reddit评论，记录了政治话语多元化趋势中一种意识形态不对称的中断，该中断大约在2022年末出现：保守派用户此前的多元化轨迹被打断，而进步派用户没有类似变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者试图检验AI技术（特别是ChatGPT）是否以及如何影响政治话语的多样性，并区分意识形态差异，同时探究这种变化是突发事件还是渐进的生态过程。

Method: 综合使用多种因果推断策略（中断时间序列ITS、双重差分DiD、回归断点RDiT、倾向得分匹配）和多种时间聚合方式；通过2,377个候选日期的每日频率置换检验评估ChatGPT阈值的显著性；构建覆盖七种模型发布的连续累积LLM指数；并进行"留驻者分析"（stayer analysis）来缩小机制范围。

Result: 不对称性在多种估计策略和时间聚合中一致；ChatGPT阈值估计仅处于第49.8百分位，说明变化是渐进而非单点断裂；连续LLM指数在二次趋势规范下仍显著；留驻者分析显示该效应在限制样本为全程活跃作者后消失，置信区间排除了全样本估计十分之一的个体内效应。

Conclusion: 机制最简洁的解释是生态层面的（社区级话语趋同）而非个体层面的AI采用，尽管数据无法将这一解释与同时期的世俗变化完全区分开来。

Abstract: I document an ideologically asymmetric break in the pre-existing diversification trend of political discourse, emerging around late 2022, using 6 million Reddit comments from two cross-partisan forums, 2019-2025. Conservative users experienced an interruption of their prior diversification trajectory; progressive users showed no comparable change. The asymmetry is consistent across estimation strategies (ITS, DiD, RDiT, propensity-score matching) and temporal aggregations. A daily-frequency permutation test over 2,377 candidate cutoff dates shows the ChatGPT threshold produces an unremarkable estimate (49.8th percentile): the shift builds gradually instead of breaking at a single date. A continuous cumulative LLM index, tracking AI exposure across seven model releases, remains significant under a quadratic trend specification that eliminates the binary estimate. A stayer analysis narrows the mechanism: the homogenization effect disappears when the sample is restricted to authors active throughout the study period, and the stayer confidence interval excludes within-author effects even a tenth the size of the full-sample estimate. The mechanism is most parsimoniously ecological (community-level discursive convergence) rather than individual-level AI adoption, though the data cannot cleanly separate this account from concurrent secular change.

</details>


### [219] [Language-Specific Gaps in AI Safety Training Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13695)
*Chialuka Prisca-Mary Onuoha,Bright Etornam Sunu,Rashidat Sikiru*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文审计了21个多语言安全基准资源、覆盖25个语言切片（20个数据集），针对豪萨语（低资源）、斯瓦希里语（中资源）、法语（高资源）三种语言，发现所谓的"多语言覆盖"声明通常经不起单语言层面的检验，存在来源、标注可靠性、可访问性、危害分类覆盖和数据复用等方面的缺口，且这些缺口部分但不完全与资源层级相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型提供者常引用覆盖十多种语言的多语言安全基准来宣称其模型对非英语用户是安全的，但这些"集合层面的覆盖声明"通常无法在单语言层面存活。作者希望揭示这种表面覆盖与实际单语言质量之间的巨大差距，并证明这些差距是可测量、可解决的，而非语言本身固有的。

Method: 作者对21个资源、25个语言切片进行了系统审计，覆盖低资源（豪萨语）、中资源（斯瓦希里语）和高资源（法语）三种语言层级。审计维度包括来源（provenance）、标注可靠性、可访问性、危害分类覆盖和数据复用。采用受控的管道内对比实验，在同一翻译管道下比较豪萨语和斯瓦希里语输出质量。另外还检查了自残和性内容类别的母语覆盖情况，并与多语言越狱鲁棒性中的单轮/多轮攻击不对称性进行结构性关联。

Result: 研究发现：豪萨语切片低于其论文自身的翻译质量接受阈值，而同一管道的斯瓦希里语输出却轻松达标；自残和性内容类别在两种非洲语言层级中完全没有母语覆盖，这是一个"总缺口"而非"渐次缺口"；缺口模式部分但不完全遵循资源层级。研究还将这些发现与多语言越狱鲁棒性中的单轮攻击大多被缓解、多轮攻击仍有效的持久不对称性联系起来。

Conclusion: 作者贡献了可复用的切片级审计方法论、跨层级实证比较，以及面向数据集创建者、模型提供者和学术场所的具体建议，旨在使"多语言覆盖"声明从"仅陈述"变为"可验证"。

Abstract: Large language model providers routinely cite multilingual safety benchmarks spanning a dozen or more languages as evidence that their models are safe for non-English-speaking users. We show that these collection-level coverage claims frequently do not survive inspection at the level of an individual language. Auditing 21 resources across 25 language slices, of which 20 count as datasets under our counting rules, spanning three languages chosen to represent low- (Hausa), mid- (Swahili), and high-resource (French) tiers, we find that gaps in provenance, annotation reliability, access, harm-taxonomy coverage, and data reuse recur in patterns that partially, but not fully, track resource level. Using a controlled within-pipeline comparison, we show a Hausa-language slice falling below its own paper's translation-quality acceptance threshold while the same pipeline's Swahili output clears the same bar comfortably; this is evidence that these gaps are measurable and addressable, not inherent. We further show that self-harm and sexual-content categories have no native-language coverage in either African-language tier we studied, a total rather than gradated gap that a purely resource-level account does not predict. We connect these findings to a documented, persistent asymmetry in multilingual jailbreak robustness (single-turn attacks largely mitigated, multi-turn attacks still effective), arguing that this asymmetry is structurally consistent with where our audit finds training and evaluation data thinnest. We contribute a reusable slice-level audit methodology, a cross-tier empirical comparison, and concrete recommendations for dataset creators, model providers, and venues aiming to make ``multilingual coverage'' claims verifiable rather than merely stated. Dataset: https://huggingface.co/datasets/ChialukaOnuoha/safety-slice-audit

</details>


### [220] [Reading Between The Lines: Modeling and Evaluating Behavioral Realism in Legal Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13712)
*Divya Vetticaden,Arya Gupta,Julian Nyarko,Megan Ma*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出WitnessSim，一个由可控法律人格驱动的证词模拟器，并建立评估框架将行为真实性与教学实用性分离评估。结果显示其在行为边界合理性、律师偏好中立性及行为动态响应方面表现良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有法律AI评估主要聚焦于事实准确性、推理能力或回应层面的合理性，但证词培训（deposition training）本质上需要律师应对动态的证人行为，这一行为维度在现有评估中被忽视。

Method: 引入WitnessSim模拟器，采用可控法律人格驱动；评估框架分离两个维度：行为真实性（通过对抗性测试、盲法律师比较、纵向行为轨迹分析来评估）和教学实用性（检验证人行为是否随提问形式和律师干预产生有意义的变化）。

Result: WitnessSim在大多数情况下能维持合理的行为边界；律师并未系统性偏好原版证词或WitnessSim生成的证词；教学测试表明证人行为能响应对问题形式和律师干预而变，同时不会完全坍缩预设的人格。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了法律模拟中行为保真度的模型，并为评估其性能提供了框架，弥补了法律AI评估中行为维度缺失的空白。

Abstract: Deposition training requires attorneys to manage dynamic witness behavior, yet legal-AI evaluations largely focus on factual accuracy, reasoning, or response-level plausibility. We introduce WitnessSim, a deposition simulator driven by controllable legal personas. We use an evaluation framework separating behavioral realism from pedagogical usefulness. We assess realism through adversarial testing, blinded attorney comparison, and analysis of longitudinal behavioral trajectories. WitnessSim generally maintained plausible behavioral boundaries, and attorneys did not systematically prefer either original testimony or WitnessSim generated testimony. Pedagogical tests showed that witness behavior changed meaningfully in response to question form and attorney intervention without uniformly collapsing the assigned persona. Together, these results showcase a model of behavioral fidelity in legal simulations, and provide a framework for evaluating its performance.

</details>


### [221] [Meteorology-driven Causal Nowcasting of Fugitive Landfill Emissions Enables Proactive Public Health Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14254)
*Timothy C. Pearce,David J. T. Smith,Alec Dobney,Alessia Freddo*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出CAIRN（Causal-Anchored Inference for Receptor Nowcasting）机器学习框架，利用常规气象数据和日历直接预测垃圾填埋场释放的硫化氢浓度，通过快慢双时间尺度组件匹配监测到的传输机制，实现社区暴露的实时预警而非事后追溯。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 垃圾填埋场的逸散性排放持续使社区暴露于有毒和恶臭气体，但现有公共卫生响应是回顾性的——只能在居民暴露后才展开调查。缺乏基于常规监测数据提前识别气象驱动因素和影响时间尺度的能力，导致无法在排放事件展开过程中即时评估社区影响。

Method: 提出CAIRN机器学习框架，其内部记忆与实际测量的时间尺度相匹配：快速组件跟踪小时级的风向传输，慢速组件跟踪多小时级的天气变化。仅使用常规气象变量和日历作为输入，无需手工特征工程。训练目标是预测气体测量值，框架可在不同监测站点和共排放甲烷气体间无改动迁移。将四个此类预报器组合形成站点级、分级警报系统。

Result: CAIRN的行为与已识别的传输机制一致，且框架可无改动迁移至第二个监测站和共排放的甲烷。组合四个预报器产生的分级警报与直接传感器网络生成的警报高度吻合，并成功追踪独立的社区恶臭投诉记录。

Conclusion: 天气驱动的实时预报能够在排放事件展开过程中估算社区影响，为公共卫生部门提供经过验证的分级干预触发机制，使暴露能够在事件期间而非事件之后得到减少。

Abstract: Fugitive emissions from waste sites increasingly expose communities to toxic and odorous gases, yet public-health responses remain largely retrospective, with episodes investigated only after residents have been exposed. Here we show that the meteorological drivers of elevated hydrogen sulphide (HS) at a long-monitored European landfill, and the timescales over which they act, can be identified directly from routine monitoring data. We introduce CAIRN (Causal-Anchored Inference for Receptor Nowcasting), a machine-learning framework whose internal memory is matched to these measured timescales: a fast component tracking hour-scale wind-borne transport and a slow component tracking multi-hour weather changes. Trained to predict gas measurements, CAIRN operates using only routine weather variables and the calendar, without hand-engineered features. Its behaviour is consistent with the identified transport mechanisms, and the framework transfers unchanged to a second monitoring station and to co-emitted methane. Combining four such nowcasters produces a site-level, tiered alert aligned with WHO odour guidance that closely reproduces the alert generated by a direct sensor network and tracks an independent record of community odour complaints. Weather-driven nowcasting can therefore estimate community impact as an emission episode unfolds, providing public-health authorities with a validated, graded trigger for intervention and enabling exposure to be reduced during events rather than after them.

</details>


### [222] [Designing Inclusive Crypto-Asset Dispute Resolution A Hybrid AI and Smart Contract Online Dispute Resolution Framework for Vulnerable Users](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14356)
*Gioia Arnone,Marco Giacalone*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出一个结合人工智能与区块链智能合约的混合型在线争议解决（ODR）框架，用于解决加密资产相关纠纷。AI主要在链下运行，支持自然语言交互、争议分类、证据整理和法律信息提供；智能合约在链上执行有限的注册、时间戳、验证、托管和结果执行功能。该框架以人为中心，强调可解释性、程序指导和人工监督，并通过加密资产扣押、交易所失败和跨境诈骗三类案例进行分析，论证其在可访问性、透明度、效率和可执行性方面的潜力，同时处理算法偏见、责任、数据保护和欧盟AI法案及MiCA法规的合规问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加密资产的快速增长产生了难以在现有法律救济机制中解决的争议，主要困难源于区块链交易的技术特征、跨境关系的复杂性以及法律规则的分裂。缺乏法律或技术专长的普通用户尤其面临权利识别、证据组织和有效救济获取的障碍，可能产生新型的数字排斥现象，尽管加密资产与金融可及性相关联。因此需要一个能够降低这些壁垒的争议解决框架。

Method: 提出混合ODR框架：AI主要在链下运作，负责自然语言交互、争议分类、证据组织和可获取的法律信息；智能合约执行有限的链上功能，包括争议注册、时间戳、验证、托管管理及适当的约定结果执行。框架以人为中心，强调可解释性、程序指导和复杂案件中的人力监督，并通过加密资产扣押、交易所失败和跨境诈骗三类争议场景进行检验。

Result: 论文论证了一个谨慎限定的混合架构可能提升可访问性、透明度、效率和可执行性，前提是自动化和人工角色被清晰界定。同时识别并回应了关键法律和治理关切，包括算法偏见、责任、数据保护、智能合约可执行性，以及欧盟AI法案和加密资产市场法规（MiCA）的合规要求。

Conclusion: 论文提出一个具有法律基础的框架，旨在为加密资产环境中的争议解决提供更具包容性和稳健性的方案，平衡自动化效率与法律合规及人权保障。

Abstract: The growing use of crypto-assets has generated disputes that sit uneasily within existing legal redress mechanisms. Their resolution is complicated by the technical features of blockchain transactions, the cross-border nature of many relationships, and fragmented legal rules. These difficulties are particularly acute for users without legal or technical expertise, who may struggle to identify their rights, organise relevant evidence, or pursue an effective remedy. Crypto-assets may therefore produce new forms of digital exclusion, despite their association with financial accessibility. This paper examines whether a hybrid Online Dispute Resolution framework combining artificial intelligence and blockchain-based smart contracts could mitigate these barriers. In the proposed model, AI operates primarily off-chain, supporting natural-language interaction, dispute classification, evidence organisation, and accessible legal information. Smart contracts perform limited on-chain functions, including dispute registration, timestamping, verification, escrow management, and, where appropriate, execution of agreed outcomes. The framework is human-centred, with emphasis on explainability, procedural guidance, and human oversight in complex cases. The model is examined through disputes involving crypto-asset seizure, exchange failures, and cross-border scams. The paper argues that a carefully delimited hybrid architecture may improve accessibility, transparency, efficiency, and enforceability, provided that automated and human roles are clearly defined. It also addresses key legal and governance concerns, including algorithmic bias, liability, data protection, smart-contract enforceability, and compliance with the EU AI Act and the Markets in Crypto-Assets Regulation. The paper thus proposes a legally grounded framework for more inclusive and robust dispute resolution in crypto-asset environments.

</details>


### [223] [Whose doctor does the AI recommend? An algorithm audit of reputation and demographic signals in large language model-assisted physician choice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14399)
*Syeda Anshrah Gillani,Mirza Samad Ahmed Baig*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文通过预设的随机化算法审计，研究了大型语言模型（LLM）作为"AI信息中介"在推荐医生时的因果驱动因素。研究发现声誉信号（评分和费用）主导推荐结果，且存在人口统计学偏差（女性及少数族裔姓名有轻微优势），但这些偏差在模型自身解释中完全不可见，因此依赖模型自我报告的透明度机制无法检测此类偏见。研究呼吁采用可重复的行为审计作为监控手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 患者越来越多地向LLM助手咨询"该看哪位医生"，使LLM成为信息中介（AI infomediaries），在规模上静默决定哪些医生被看见。这种推荐系统可能引入系统性偏差，而现有透明度机制依赖模型自我解释，可能无法检测实际偏差，因此需要因果审计来确定什么因素真正驱动了推荐。

Method: 采用预设的随机化算法审计：七个模型（六个开源权重模型加gpt-4o-mini）在3,024个选择集中从五个合成的家庭医学医生卡片中选择，卡片属性独立随机化，结合三个人格人设、九个提示改写、九个实验组，共产生40,068个评分响应。性别和种族通过姓名信号传达（遵循通信审计方法），测试模型在给出推荐后的理由表述。

Result: 声誉信号主导：评分从3.9升至4.7使选择概率增加31.4个百分点（pp）；费用从$90升至$190使选择概率降低20.0 pp。人口统计学平价被拒绝，但方向与人类审计研究预测相反：女性姓名获得+2.5 pp，拉丁裔、南亚裔和黑人姓名比白人姓名获得+1.3至+2.9 pp的优势（折合每次就诊$7-$14的费用等价），无内容的首位位置值$11。然而模型在最多0.03%的陈述理由中提及性别或种族，在0.39%的试验中选择弃权，这些效应在模型自身解释中不可见。一个推理模型直接未通过预设的可审计性门控。

Conclusion: LLM作为AI信息中介存在系统性且因果可测量的推荐偏差，但这些偏差无法通过模型自我报告检测。冻结的审计设计具有可重复性，任何新模型都可以用相同的刺激进行评估，因此周期性行为审计（而非自我报告解释）才是适合此目的的监控技术。

Abstract: Patients increasingly ask large language model (LLM) assistants which doctor to see, making these systems AI infomediaries: algorithms that intermediate one person's choice among other people and thereby decide, silently and at scale, which physicians become visible. We report a prespecified randomized algorithm audit of what causally moves those recommendations. Seven models (six open-weight; gpt-4o-mini) each chose among five synthetic family-medicine physician cards whose attributes were independently randomized across 3,024 choice sets, three patient personas, nine prompt paraphrases and nine experimental arms, yielding 40,068 scored responses; gender and ethnicity were signaled through names following correspondence-audit methodology. Reputation signals dominate: raising a rating from 3.9 to 4.7 increases choice probability by 31.4 percentage points (pp), and raising the fee from $90 to $190 lowers it by 20.0 pp. Demographic parity is rejected, but not in the direction human audit studies predict: female-signaled names gain 2.5 pp, and Hispanic-, South-Asian- and Black-signaled names gain 1.3-2.9 pp over White-signaled names, tilts worth $7-$14 per visit in fee-equivalent terms, and a content-free first-listed position is worth $11. Yet models mentioned gender or ethnicity in at most 0.03% of their stated reasons and abstained in 0.39% of trials, so these effects are invisible in the models' own explanations, and transparency obligations relying on model self-report would not detect them. One reasoning model failed the prespecified auditability gate outright. The frozen design makes the audit repeatable: any new model can be assessed against identical stimuli, making recurring behavioural audit, rather than self-reported explanation, the monitoring technology fit for purpose.

</details>


### [224] [Estimating the growth in emissions from AI data centres](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14421)
*Wim Vanderbauwhede*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文使用最先进的生命周期评估模型，量化了AI数据中心扩张带来的CO2排放增长，指出AI行业推广的场景将导致排放急剧上升，且隐含碳（embodied carbon）占比显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体AI（agentic AI）的兴起正推动数据中心前所未有的扩张，但目前缺乏对由此产生的整体CO2排放的严格量化评估。作者旨在填补这一空白，对AI数据中心的排放进行严谨的量化分析。

Method: 采用最先进的生命周期评估（LCA）模型，分别计算AI服务器的运营排放（operational emissions）和隐含排放（embodied emissions），并结合IEA和McKinsey的情景预测数据来估算AI数据中心扩张所带来的整体CO2排放增长。

Result: 研究发现，AI行业推行的扩张情景将导致整体排放急剧上升，且隐含碳（服务器制造等环节的碳排放）占总排放的比例相当可观，不容忽视。

Conclusion: AI数据中心的扩张将带来严重的碳排放问题，其中隐含碳成分显著，需要在推动AI发展的同时重视其环境足迹和可持续性。

Abstract: The advent of agentic AI is driving an unprecedented growth in data centre expansion. Using state-of-the-art life cycle assessment models for operational and embodied emissions from AI servers, we estimate the ensuing growth in overall CO2 emissions of AI data centres. The main contribution of this work is a rigorous quantification of emissions arising from projected AI data centre expansion using scenarios by the IEA and McKinsey. We show that the scenarios promoted by the AI industry would result in a dramatic rise in overall emissions and that the embodied carbon component is considerable.

</details>


<div id='eess.SY'></div>

# eess.SY [[Back]](#toc)

### [225] [Consistent Model Chasing Is Minimax Optimal: The Exact Value of Scalar Adversarial Adaptive Control under Large Parametric Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13651)
*Dimitar Ho*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文精确求解了一个对抗性扰动下自适应控制的根本问题：对未知极点a∈[-Δ,Δ]的标量系统，最小最坏情形峰值（博弈值）为γ*(Δ)=1+Δ，并证明确定性等价死区控制（中点一致性估计）为最优策略，首次为"一致模型追踪"架构提供了精确最优性证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管自适应控制在稳定性、增益界和遗憾率方面已有大量理论成果，但从未有人精确求解过含任意大小参数不确定性的自适应控制问题的最坏情形峰值博弈值。本文旨在填补这一空白，确定该基本问题的最优值及其达到策略。

Method: 将控制器表述为"预言机-选择器"组合架构（θ_t = -u_t/x_t），证明所有因果控制器均可分解为此形式；通过集合成员一致性估计框架，将最优策略钉扎为一致性区间中点的确定性等价死区控制；并逐一量化分析标准方法（探测、承诺、乐观、遗憾界）的失败方式。

Result: 博弈值为γ*(Δ)=1+Δ，其中1是扰动的不可约成本，Δ是不可避免的辨识尖峰的精确成本；最优策略是不含探索机制的确定性等价中点死区控制，其学习完全被动。

Conclusion: 该结果首次为一致模型追踪作为对抗自适应控制设计原则提供了精确最优性证书；标准工具（探测、承诺、乐观、遗憾证书）均被量化证明失效。

Abstract: We solve exactly a fundamental problem of adaptive control against adversarial disturbances: regulate the scalar system $x_{t+1} = ax_t + u_t + w_t$, $x_0=0$, $\|w\|_\infty \le 1$, where the constant pole $a \in [-Δ, Δ]$ is unknown in sign and magnitude and $Δ$ is arbitrarily large. Elementary as the system looks, the least worst-case peak $\|x\|_\infty$ that a causal controller can guarantee against an adversarial pair $(a, w)$ (the value of this game) has, to our knowledge, never been determined for any adaptive control problem with parametric uncertainty of arbitrary size under this criterion; existing theory supplies stability certificates, gain bounds, and regret rates, not the value. That value is $γ^\star(Δ) = 1 + Δ$ for every $Δ>0$. The summand $1$ is the irreducible price of the disturbance, and $Δ$ the exact price of a single, unavoidable identification spike. The optimal policy is certainty-equivalent deadbeat control at the midpoint of the set-membership consistent interval, an instance of the robust oracle $\times$ consistent model chasing architecture. The architecture is forced, not merely sufficient: writing $θ_t := -u_t/x_t$ exhibits every causal controller as an oracle-selector composition, and optimality pins the selector to the midpoint at the critical histories. The standard tools, classical and modern, each fail quantifiably: probing is punished before it pays, commitment is fatal at sub-disturbance excitation once adaptation is necessary, optimism degenerates to tie-breaking or pays asymptotically at least twice the optimum, and regret certificates are blind to the worst-case peak in both directions. The optimal law contains no exploration mechanism, its learning purely passive. These results give the first exact optimality certificate for consistent model chasing as a design principle for adversarial adaptive control.

</details>


### [226] [Control Barrier--Value Functions under Partial Observability: Safety Guarantees via Conformal Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13819)
*Niloofar Jahanshahi,Mo Chen*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文针对部分可观测非线性控制系统，将控制屏障-值函数(CBVF)框架扩展到基于估计状态的场景，利用共形预测获得估计误差界，并据此建立基于估计器空间的CBVF安全证书，推导有限时域概率安全保证，提出QP在线安全滤波器，并在部分可观测避障案例中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CBVF框架假设完全状态信息可用，但实际系统中往往只能获得部分观测，控制只能基于估计状态。需要将安全分析与控制器综合扩展到部分可观测场景，同时提供具有统计保证的安全性验证方法。

Method: 1) 使用估计器获取状态估计；2) 应用共形预测对估计误差进行定量刻画，得到用户指定误覆盖率水平下的误差界；3) 将该误差界融入估计器空间的安全分析中，定义针对部分可观测系统的CBVF安全证书；4) 推导真实系统状态的有限时域概率安全保证；5) 提出基于QP的在线安全滤波器，对控制仿射且扰动有界的系统实时强制执行CBVF安全条件。

Result: 在部分可观测避障案例中验证了所提框架的有效性，表明该方法能够为部分可观测非线性系统提供具有概率保证的安全控制。

Conclusion: 该工作将CBVF框架成功推广至部分可观测系统，通过共形预测提供统计意义的误差界，实现了在不确定估计条件下仍能保证真实系统状态安全的控制器综合方法。

Abstract: This paper studies safety analysis and controller synthesis for partially observable nonlinear control systems. We extend the control barrier--value function (CBVF) framework, which combines Hamilton--Jacobi reachability and control barrier functions, to settings where full state information is not available and control is based on an estimated state. Given an estimator, we apply conformal prediction to the estimation error and obtain an error bound at a user-chosen miscoverage level. We incorporate this bound into the estimator-space safety analysis and define a CBVF-based safety certificate for partially observable systems. We then derive a finite-horizon probabilistic safety guarantee for the true system state. Finally, we propose a QP-based online safety filter for systems affine in the control and disturbance, whose solution enforces the CBVF safety condition in real time against bounded disturbance. The proposed framework is illustrated on a partially observable obstacle-avoidance case study.

</details>


### [227] [Hierarchical Sensor-Spoofing Defence Framework for Networked DC Microgrids via Cyber-Physical Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13823)
*Mengxiang Liu,Xin Zhang,Shiyi Zhao,Ruilong Deng*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一种分层框架，通过多层网络物理协调来检测并缓解网络化微电网（NMGs）中的传感器欺骗攻击。该框架仅在关键点（各微电网的本地PCC）部署物理防护技术，并基于安全传感器读数采用网络检测/缓解算法，通过MG-DER协调的主动检测方案触发参数扰动以有效披露智能传感器欺骗攻击，随后激活基于MG-DER协调的缓解方案来递归且准确地估计传感器偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对传感器欺骗攻击的防御方法（物理防护与网络检测/缓解）各有优劣，但缺乏对跨DER多层高效协调的系统性研究，难以在成本、安全性和性能之间取得平衡。

Method: 提出分层框架：(1) 仅在关键点（微电网本地PCC）部署物理防护技术以降低成本；(2) 基于安全传感器读数采用网络检测/缓解算法以应对DER中的传感器欺骗攻击；(3) 采用MG-DER协调的主动检测方案，通过策略性触发参数扰动有效披露智能传感器欺骗攻击；(4) 激活基于MG-DER协调的缓解方案，递归且准确地估计传感器偏差。

Result: 在网络物理直流NMG测试台上的实验证实了该框架在多种攻击场景下的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的分层多层网络物理协调框架能够在控制成本的同时，通过在关键点部署物理防护并利用网络层面的主动检测与缓解算法，有效检测和缓解网络化微电网中的传感器欺骗攻击。

Abstract: In parallel to the cyber attack that manipulates the reference points of distributed energy resources (DERs) by maliciously accessing the remote monitoring and control system, the vulnerability of voltage/current sensors to electromagnetic interference (EMI) in the physical domain has been widely discussed. Existing research efforts against sensor spoofing attacks can be classified into physical prevention and cyber detection/mitigation. These defence methods each have strengths and weaknesses in balancing cost, security, and performance in a single DER, yet systematic research on their multi-layer efficient coordination across DERs remains limited. Towards this end, this paper proposes a hierarchical framework to detect and mitigate sensor spoofing attacks in networked microgrids (NMGs) via {multi-layer cyber-physical coordination}. It requires only to deploy physical prevention technologies at critical points, i.e., the local points of common coupling (PCC) of MGs, such that cyber detection/mitigation algorithms can be adopted based on the secured sensor readings to counter sensor spoofing attacks in DERs. The framework employs an MG-DER coordinated proactive detection scheme to strategically trigger parameter perturbations, under which the intelligent sensor spoofing attacks can be {effectively} disclosed. Afterwards, mitigation schemes based on MG-DER coordination are activated to recursively and accurately estimate sensor biases. Experiments on a cyber-physical DC NMG testbed confirm the framework's effectiveness across diverse attack scenarios.

</details>


### [228] [Real-Time In-Domain Congestion Control for the LWR Traffic Model via Control Barrier Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13841)
*Zehang Zhu,Brian Block,Stephanie Stockar*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于控制屏障函数的方法，用于Lighthill-Whitham-Richards交通模型的实时域内拥塞控制。通过将交通拥塞建模为分布式安全控制问题，并利用离散化和KKT分析转化为高维二次规划，开发了利用结构特性的原始-对偶活动集算法，在保证收敛性的前提下实现实时安全控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交通拥塞是现代城市面临的重大挑战，LWR交通模型是经典的宏观交通流模型。传统的拥塞控制方法往往难以同时满足实时性要求和严格的安全约束，因此需要一种能够在域内（in-domain）实时求解的安全控制方法。

Method: 1) 将交通拥塞问题表述为分布式安全控制问题，构建无限维优化问题；2) 通过空间离散化将问题转化为有限维，并利用Karush-Kuhn-Tucker (KKT)分析将其转换为高维二次规划；3) 提出一种利用问题结构的原始-对偶活动集算法（primal-dual active set algorithm）来高效求解，并给出收敛性保证。

Result: 通过使用不同标称控制器（nominal controllers）的数值仿真，验证了所提出方法的有效性和实时可行性。

Conclusion: 基于控制屏障函数并结合结构利用型活动集算法，可以在实时条件下有效解决LWR交通模型的域内拥塞控制问题，为交通安全管理提供了一种计算高效且具有理论保证的解决方案。

Abstract: This paper presents a control barrier function-based method for real-time in-domain congestion control of the Lighthill-Whitham-Richards traffic model. Traffic congestion is formulated as a distributed safety control problem, leading to a infinite-dimensional optimization problem. Through the discretization and Karush-Kuhn-Tucker (KKT) analysis, the problem is converted into a high-dimensional quadratic program. A structure-exploiting primal-dual active set algorithm is then developed to compute the safe control input in real time, with convergence guarantees. Numerical simulations with different nominal controllers demonstrate the effectiveness and real-time feasibility of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [229] [Feedback-Guided DNN-Based Controller Fusion for Robust Fixed-Parameter Active Noise Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14061)
*Lu Bai,Yiming He,Xiaofeng Nan,Kai Chen,Jing Lu*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一种反馈引导的DNN控制器融合框架，用于鲁棒的固定参数主动噪声控制(ANC)。该方法结合因果WaveNet控制器与反馈引导的专家混合(MoE)模块，通过门控网络根据当前声学条件估计多个预训练FIR专家网络的权重，无需在线参数更新即可适应变化的声学环境。模型完全因果，支持逐样本流式推理，计算负载均匀分布以降低峰值负载。耳机ANC实验表明在低频段具有显著降噪效果，且1-8 kHz频段噪声放大可忽略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自适应ANC方法可能不稳定或发散，限制实际部署；而固定参数控制器在噪声特性和声学路径变化时性能会下降。因此需要一种既稳定（固定参数）又能适应变化声学条件的鲁棒ANC方案。

Method: 提出反馈引导的DNN控制器融合框架，包含两个核心组件：(1)因果WaveNet控制器用于时域噪声建模与抵消；(2)反馈引导的MoE模块，通过门控网络根据当前声学条件动态估计多个预训练FIR专家的组合权重。该系统无需在线更新参数，完全因果并支持逐样本流式推理，计算负载均匀分配以缓解峰值计算压力。

Result: 在耳机ANC实验中，该方法实现了显著的低频噪声抑制效果，同时在1-8 kHz范围内噪声放大现象可忽略不计，验证了其在变化声学条件下的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该框架成功弥合了固定参数控制器的稳定性与自适应控制器的适应性之间的鸿沟，为实际ANC部署提供了一种无需在线更新即可适应变化声学环境的鲁棒解决方案。

Abstract: In active noise control (ANC) systems, adaptive approaches may suffer from instability or divergence, limiting their practical deployment. Consequently, fixed-parameter controllers are widely adopted, but their performance degrades under varying noise characteristics and acoustic path conditions. This paper proposes a feedback-guided DNN-based controller fusion framework for robust fixed-parameter ANC. The proposed method combines a causal WaveNet controller with a feedback-guided mixture-of-experts (MoE) module, where a gating network estimates the weights of multiple pre-trained FIR experts according to the current acoustic condition. The proposed approach improves robustness to varying acoustic conditions without online parameter updating. Furthermore, the model is fully causal and supports sample-wise streaming inference, with computational costs evenly distributed across sampling points to reduce peak computational load. Experimental results on headphone ANC demonstrate substantial low-frequency noise reduction with negligible noise amplification over 1-8 kHz.

</details>


### [230] [A Temporal Barrier Framework for Collision Avoidance in Multi-Agent Autonomous Aerial Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14239)
*Benedikt Barthel Sorensen,Mitchell Black,Erfaun Noorani,Themistoklis Sapsis*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出对抗性碰撞时间（aTTC）风险度量及其对应的控制障碍函数（CBF）实现。aTTC量化了周围任意智能体在假设对抗意图下最快到达本体的时间，并以时间为障碍而不是距离或速度。通过可微神经网络代理实现实时计算，在3D独立追逐和编队飞行仿真中，相比高阶基于距离的CBF基线，航点进度提升两倍，碰撞率降低一半。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在动态、不确定且可能对抗的环境中运行自主飞行器编队，需要既可靠又具有选择性的安全协议，使智能体能近距离飞行同时推进任务目标。现有基于距离或速度的CBF方法可能过于保守或反应迟缓，缺乏前瞻性。

Method: 1) 定义对抗性碰撞时间（aTTC）风险度量；2) 将aTTC嵌入CBF框架，定义时间域的障碍函数；3) 利用可微神经网络代理实现aTTC的实时计算；4) 在标准CBF二次规划中集成。

Result: 在长时程3D独立追逐和编队飞行仿真中，aTTC-CBF相比高阶基于距离的CBF基线，航点进度提升至两倍，碰撞率降低一半。

Conclusion: aTTC-CBF通过时间域障碍设计实现了前瞻性的安全行为，兼顾安全性与任务效率，在仿真中显著优于距离基CBF基线。

Abstract: Operating teams of autonomous aircraft in dynamic, uncertain, and potentially adversarial environments requires safety protocols that are reliable yet selective, and allow agents to fly in close proximity while making progress toward mission objectives. We introduce adversarial time-to-collision (aTTC), a risk metric that quantifies, for a given agent, how quickly any surrounding agent could reach it assuming adversarial intent. We embed aTTC into the control barrier function (CBF) framework, defining the barrier directly in time rather than distance or velocity. The resulting aTTC-CBF is inherently anticipatory: agents modulate their own velocity based not on whether a peer is on a collision course, but on how quickly one could reach collision given its dynamical constraints. A differentiable neural-network surrogate makes the aTTC computable in real time within a standard CBF quadratic program. Across long time-horizon simulations of 3D independent-pursuit and formation-flight scenarios, the aTTC-CBF achieves up to twice the waypoint progress at half the collision rate of a higher-order distance-based CBF baseline.

</details>


### [231] [Diagonalizable Directed Laplacians by Positive Arc-Weight Design for Master Stability Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14439)
*Aandrew Baggio Sahaya Arokiadoss*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种使弱连通有向图的加权入度拉普拉斯矩阵可对角化的构造方法，通过提取根强连通分量的生成有向无环图并分配正权重，保证非零特征值互异，从而恢复主稳定函数（MSF）变分方程的完全解耦形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统主稳定函数（MSF）理论依赖可对角化的网络拉普拉斯矩阵，因为可对角化才能将变分方程分解为独立方程。然而有向图的拉普拉斯矩阵不一定可对角化，这限制了MSF方法在一般有向网络上的应用。

Method: 首先提取一个弱连通的生成有向无环图，该图在每个根强连通分量中恰好有一个源顶点；然后为正权重赋值使得其余所有顶点的加权入度两两不同；最后将原始有向图的剩余弧赋予一个公共的充分小的正权重。在扰动下非零特征值保持两两不同，零特征值是半单的，其重数等于根强连通分量的数量。

Result: 该构造使得拉普拉斯矩阵具有完整的特征向量集，恢复了MSF变分方程的完全解耦形式。此外，论文还给出了基于判别式的准则来计算公共弧权重的可行区间。

Conclusion: 每个弱连通有向图都可以通过适当的弧加权实现其入度拉普拉斯矩阵可对角化，从而有效推广了MSF框架到更一般的非对称有向网络。

Abstract: The standard master stability function (MSF) formulation has traditionally relied on a diagonalizable network Laplacian, since diagonalizability allows the variational equations to be decomposed into independent equations. Directed Laplacians, however, need not be diagonalizable. We show that every weakly connected digraph admits a strictly positive arc weighting for which its weighted in-degree Laplacian is diagonalizable. Our construction first extracts a weakly connected spanning directed acyclic graph having exactly one source vertex in each root strongly connected component and assigns positive weights so that the weighted indegrees of all remaining vertices are pairwise distinct. The remaining arcs of the original digraph are then assigned a common sufficiently small positive weight. The nonzero eigenvalues remain pairwise distinct under this perturbation, while the zero eigenvalue is semisimple, with multiplicity equal to the number of root strongly connected components. Consequently, the resulting Laplacian admits a complete set of eigenvectors and restores the fully decoupled form of the MSF variational equations. We further give a discriminant-based criterion for computing an admissible interval of the common arc weight.

</details>


### [232] [Optimal Scheduling of Road Maintenance Jobs Considering Impact on Traffic Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14491)
*Charitha Nandepu,Lohitha Kalepu,Gabriele Ciavarella,SangWoo Park*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究利用数据驱动的替代模型直接从OD需求近似均衡弧流量，以解决网络级维护规划中反复求解均衡交通流分配的高计算成本问题，并在新泽西州纽瓦克地区的真实交通数据上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络级维护规划需要反复评估道路容量减少下的均衡交通流，虽然均衡交通分配模型已很成熟，但重复求解计算成本极高，难以嵌入维护调度优化问题中。

Method: 采用数据驱动的替代模型，以优化求解的均衡解作为真实标签（ground truth），直接从OD需求预测均衡弧流量。

Result: 基于新泽西州纽瓦克地区真实交通数据的案例研究表明，该方法能够有效地作为可扩展的基础模块，用于未来的维护调度框架。

Conclusion: 数据驱动替代模型为解决均衡交通流反复求解的计算瓶颈提供了一种可扩展的有效方案，可作为维护调度框架的构建基础。

Abstract: Network-level maintenance planning requires repeated evaluations of equilibrium traffic flows under road capacity reductions. While equilibrium traffic assignment models are well established, their repeated solution quickly becomes computationally prohibitive and challenging to embed within maintenance scheduling problems. This paper investigates data-driven surrogate models that approximate equilibrium arc flows directly from origin-destination demand, using optimization-based equilibrium solutions as ground truth. A real-world case study based on traffic data from the Newark, New Jersey area demonstrates the effectiveness of the proposed approach as a scalable building block for future maintenance scheduling frameworks.

</details>


<div id='q-fin.ST'></div>

# q-fin.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [233] [Dependence-Informed Sparse Neural Architecture for Stock Return Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14323)
*Hongyu Lin,Yulin Chen,Yuanrong Wang,Antonio Briola,Tomaso Aste*

Main category: q-fin.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出使用最大滤波团森林(MFCF)估计公司特征间的依赖关系，并将其团结构映射为同调神经网络(HNN)。该网络仅需一个参数（最大团大小K）控制架构复杂度，且具有清晰的图论含义。在1987-2016年美国股票超额收益的年度样本外预测中，HNN与三层全连接基准网络预测精度相当，但横截面排序更准确，且参数数量减少了约80倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统神经网络用于股票收益预测时，深度和隐藏层宽度等超参数难以与金融解释建立联系。作者希望通过数据驱动的特征依赖结构来指导网络架构设计，使网络结构本身具有可解释性。

Method: 先用MFCF估计94个公司特征之间的依赖关系，得到图结构和团划分；然后将团的拓扑结构映射为HNN的架构——最大团大小K决定特征交互的最高阶数，过滤图决定网络的深度、层宽和稀疏连接。训练前架构即由数据决定，无需单独选择的超参数。

Result: HNN在池化预测精度上与三层全连接基准相当，横截面排序更准确，参数数量减少约80倍。两项结构性消融实验表明，稀疏连接和特征分组估计均对排序优势有贡献，且经多重检验校正后仍显著。

Conclusion: HNN提供了一种实用且可解释的方法，将公司特征间的估计依赖关系融入神经网络架构设计，在保持预测性能的同时大幅降低参数复杂度，并赋予架构以金融含义。

Abstract: Using neural networks for stock return prediction typically requires choices about depth and hidden-layer width that are difficult to connect to financial interpretation. We study an alternative: estimate dependence among firm characteristics with a Maximally Filtered Clique Forest (MFCF), then map its clique structure to a Homological Neural Network (HNN). The MFCF maximum clique size K is the only parameter controlling architectural complexity, and it has a clear graphical meaning: it bounds the number of characteristics in each maximal clique and hence the highest interaction order the network can represent. The filtered graph then fixes the neural network's depth, layer widths, and sparse connections before training, in place of a separately chosen depth and width sequence. We apply two HNN variants to annual out-of-sample forecasts of U.S. stock excess returns from 1987 to 2016 using 94 firm characteristics. The HNN models match a three-hidden-layer benchmark on pooled predictive accuracy, rank the cross-section more accurately, and use roughly 80 times fewer parameters than a fully connected network with the same induced layer widths. Two structural ablations indicate that both the sparse connectivity and the estimated grouping of characteristics contribute to the ranking advantage, and both effects remain significant after correcting for multiple testing. These findings show that HNNs offer a practical and interpretable way to incorporate estimated dependence among firm characteristics into neural architecture design.

</details>
